2026.11.11 早报

覆盖窗口：2026-11-10T06:00:00+08:00 → 2026-11-11T06:00:00+08:00 · 更新时间：2026.11.11 06:34 北京时间 · 323 条

窗口：2026-11-10 06:00 至 2026-11-11 06:00（北京时间，UTC+8）｜监控名单 134 个账号｜所有时间均为北京时间。

Executive Summary｜核心结论

本期窗口为 2026-11-10 06:00 至 2026-11-11 06:00（北京时间，UTC+8）。其中 11 月 10 日 06:00 至 21:30 与本日晚报《2026.11.10 - X Digest - 晚报》窗口重叠：Anthropic IPO 路演条款与供应商认购、S-1 短期算力、台积电 10 月营收、TPU 8tx/8i Max、出口管制传闻以及 AI 信用/电力长约已在晚报出现，本期仍完整收录以保持 24 小时覆盖；增量主线在 10 日 21:30 至 11 日 06:00（约对应美东周一早盘至下午）：（1）Google 正式推送 Gemini 4 Carbon，Perplexity 给出“能力接近 Fable 5.2、便宜 2.5 倍”的分发比较，第三方评测显示 Fable 的更高得分往往伴随显著更高的任务成本；（2）CoreWeave 三季度营收与全年指引略超预期、backlog 约 1325 亿美元，同时 10 月 CPI 略高于预期；（3）企业入口层出现 Stripe 收购 OpenRouter、Box 与 Anthropic 打通文件读写、Claude 进攻 Office 控制平面等动作；（4）关键词抽样转述 The Information 的实验室收入数字、摩根士丹利下调海力士长期利润率假设，以及对 Sandisk“回购一半公司”说法的澄清。以下“分析/含义”为本摘要的综合判断；凡标注“据报/传闻/作者观点”的内容均来自 X 帖子或其转述的媒体报道，未经独立核实，数字均以原帖为准。

一、Gemini 4 Carbon 正式落地：分发与任务成本，开始对冲 Fable 的“更强”

事实层：@sundarpichai 宣布 Gemini 4 Carbon 今日推送，定位于持续、长周期任务（复杂软件工程、网络安全、科学发现），经 Gemini API / Google AI Studio 和企业版 Gemini 全球提供，并向 Ultra 订阅用户在 Antigravity、Gemini App 与搜索 AI Mode 开放。@demishassabis 以逆向工程其原版 Theme Park 并移植到网页作为演示。Google 还展示 AlphaProof Code：用 Carbon 对 Signal 协议底层代码做形式化验证，称生成超过 600 万行经机器检查的 Lean 证明，数年手工验证被压缩到数周，约 1% 代码库需人工审阅，结果将开源。@TianfuF（Google 侧账号）称 Carbon“在真实世界应用中已经绰绰有余，但已经不再是我们最好的内部模型”。@AravSrinivas：Perplexity 向所有 Pro 和 Max 用户推送 Carbon，称在 agentic 研究任务上能力与 Fable 5.2 相近、便宜 2.5 倍；被问及扩大范围时称“很贵，目前很难扩到 Max 以外”。

成本与评测（第三方，非官方审计）：@SemiAnalysis_ 补充其 GDPx 读法——Fable 5.2 因更强“执着”、爱拉子代理，在部分任务上 token 效率很低：材料科学任务成本较 GPT-6 Astra 高 89%、输出 token 为 4.3 倍，得分基本相同；与 Carbon 的价差达 7.5 倍；金融任务输出 token 为 Opus 5.5 的 2.3 倍，部分得分还略差；即便在软件工程上得分几乎达到 Opus 5.5 的三倍，平均每任务成本也大约按同样倍数上升。此前重叠时段他们已指出：相对 Fable 5.1，5.2 单价降 20%，但平均每任务成本升 31%（67.16→88.15 美元），并认为这是“5.2 而非 5.5 量级”的边际改进。@ArtificialAnlys 在同一 MKA 任务上：Carbon（high）约 76 轮、一小时、约 8 美元；Fable 5.2（max）224 轮、约三小时、约 68 美元——Fable 幻灯片得分更高（87% 对 72%）。关键词抽样中 @asuan_ai 转述标价：Carbon 11 月 30 日前 2/10 美元、之后 4/20 美元，对 Fable 的 8/40 美元（该账号整理，非公司备案）。产品侧还有 @kimmonismus 转述的 Gemini Robotics 2.5（称在 200 多项机器人任务上测试），以及 Fable 5.2 在 Remote Labor Index 上完成 41.3% 的项目（5.1 为 17.9%）——能力与成本要放在同一张图上读。

含义：Carbon 的投资含义不只是“又一个模型发布”，而是 Google 把长周期代理能力送进 API、搜索和企业分发，并用更低的任务成本去打刚刚开始路演的 Anthropic。若 Perplexity 的 2.5 倍价差和 SemiAnalysis / AA 的单任务成本差在实际工作负载中可复现，企业预算会从“买最强模型”转向“单位工作成本”，这对 ANT 的标价（8/40）和“更执着所以更贵”的产品策略构成近端压力，对 GOOGL 则是把 TPU 生态之外的模型层份额也写进收入故事。反过来，Fable 在部分交付质量（如幻灯片、RLI）上仍领先，TianfuF 又暗示 Carbon 并非 Google 内部最高水平——竞争还没定格。不要把演示、单一基准或个人测试写成全面替代。跟踪：官方 API 价目是否与转述一致、Perplexity 是否把 Carbon 扩出 Max、以及 ANT 在 IPO 前是否再发更强/更便宜的版本。

二、Anthropic IPO：定价下修与供应商认购仍是主线，S-1 与新的收入转述把利润和增长拆开

路演与供应商（重叠时段，转述未经独立核实）：@kimmonismus 称拟议 IPO 已启动路演，拟发行 8.25 亿股、定价 70–78 美元，完全稀释估值约 1.24–1.38 万亿美元，低于此前约 1.5 万亿预期。@ScroogeCap 梳理估值路径：8 月约 2.0–2.2 万亿（约 30x run rate）→ 10 月中 1.4–1.5 万亿（约 22–23x）→ 现共识靠近 1 万亿，认为是为避免类似 $SPCX 的上市后抛压、向 5 月融资轮靠拢；另转述路透（引备案）称希望募资 693 亿美元。@Loris_Dalleau / @nodiligence：七家供应商按 IPO 价认购约 260 亿美元（谷歌、Nvidia 各 100 亿，AMD 25 亿，Broadcom、Micron、微软各 10 亿，台积电 5 亿），约占新发行股份的 30%。@nodiligence 进一步援引合同敞口：谷歌在 2033 年前至少有 1213 亿美元云容量应付（不论是否使用），另预期 2028 年起再向其采购 1250–1500 亿美元 TPU；Broadcom 租约应收 1612 亿美元；微软至 2033 年至少 472 亿美元；亚马逊未参与此次认购，但 4 月协议已允许后续最多再向其出售 150 亿美元股票。按其中位价约 74 美元，股权价值约 9680 亿美元（计入全部期权/授予则约 1.33 万亿），与 5 月一轮约 9650 亿美元接近；供应商股份不在承销商锁定期内（出售仍需注册）。

S-1 算力与新增转述：@jukan05 转述短期租用“目前”显著贵于多年期合约，三季度 50 亿美元、前九个月 65 亿美元；营业利润从二季度盈利 9.70 亿美元转为三季度亏损 5.70 亿美元，公司归因于 50 亿美元短期算力与 20 亿美元非现金捐赠匹配费用。作者还给出短期合约最低付款、收入/算力比下降等估算（详见原帖）。增量方面，关键词抽样 @TekonBurns（约 3400 粉）与名单内 @AndrewCurran_ 均转述 The Information：Anthropic 三季度收入近 170 亿美元；OpenAI 三季度约 100 亿美元、环比约 +50%（二季度 17%），驱动被指向 Astra API 与广告，OpenAI 成本数字未披露。这是媒体转述，不是两家公司的正式财报。@TechFundies 明确把 OpenAI 的年度现金消耗轨迹称作最大盲点，并称 ANT 的增长与财务“显然没问题”——这是提问，不是新披露。@AndrewCurran_ 还称 Anthropic 再次更新 S-1、已达到 Responsible Scaling Policy 阈值，并转述公司预测 2027 年 11 月 95% 的研发将被完全自动化（AL5）——属公司前瞻，不是已实现的生产率。@bgurley 借 Jaron Lanier 的纽约时报文章批评 Anthropic 以“对齐”而非控制/约束做安全，认为其不应靠近监管制定；这是投资人观点，不是监管决定。

含义：把“收入仍在加速”（若 TI 数字属实，ANT 单季已近 170 亿）和“经营利润被短期算力打成亏损”放在一起，说明 IPO 卖的是增长和长期云/TPU 合同，不是近端利润率。供应商认购降低发行失败风险，但公开市场买的是股票，供应商买的是“合同上的廉价门票”（260 亿不足其已承诺支出约 9009 亿美元的 3%）。@aleabitoreddit 指出 NVDA 约跌 25%、市值蒸发近 2 万亿美元的背景下，以约 1 万亿量级定价 IPO 时点尴尬——这是交易观察，不是发行已被推迟的证据。跟踪：S-1 后续修订、Direct 上线后短期算力是否回落、锁定期外供应商减持，以及 TI 收入数字能否被正式披露印证。

三、需求数据仍强，砍单与“台积电做存储”仍是传闻；存储的长期利润率假设开始被下调

已发生的需求（公司披露/法定月报，数字以原帖为准）：@dnystedt：台积电 10 月营收 NT$5868.9 亿（约 184.6 亿美元，按公司四季度汇率指引折算），同比 +59.7%，刷新 8 月纪录；1–10 月同比 +43.3% 至 NT$4.49 万亿，仍跟踪全年约 44% 指引；四季度指引 523–543 亿美元。@SKundojjala 美元口径：10 月约 182.36 亿美元、环比 +13%，连续第 8 个历史新高月；1–10 月 1411 亿美元、+40.1%；若 11–12 月持平约 1776 亿美元（+45%），但历史上这两月环比下降，要完成 44% 指引需要好于季节性。台湾 10 月半导体出口同比 +63.0% 至 311.7 亿美元。@FundaAI 认为 10 月营收完全在预期之内，并把“Nvidia 考虑削减台积电订单”的传闻视为纯猜测，即使 Rubin 因 HBM 技术问题略有延期，他们也不认为订单已被削减。@jukan05 / @dnystedt 转述的台媒砍单传闻仍未向信源确认，不应写成事实。

Google 定制硅与电力半导体（重叠时段）：TPU 8tx / 8i Max 把 HBM 带宽 8.6→17.2 TB/s、容量 288→384GB；@PhotonCap 记录 AMD EPYC 为 TPU 8tx 主机供电，Broadcom 合作至 2031，并认为 Google 在搭一整条 AI 半导体生态。英飞凌 2026 财年营收 163.5 亿欧元、+12%，2027 财年指引约 200 亿欧元，数据中心指引 40 亿欧元，AI 电力 2026 财年 17 亿欧元、同比翻倍以上。HBM5（三星 Hot Chips，@SemiAnalysis_）在不增加 2048 引脚的前提下把单引脚速率提到超过 28 Gbps，但 PHY 功耗可超过 HBM4E 的 4 倍。

新的存储/代工传闻与卖方假设（需折扣）：关键词 @mafolebaraka 转述 The Information：Nvidia 据报要求台积电起草制造 3D DRAM 的计划，台积电称自己仍是逻辑公司（采集文本被截断，详见原帖）。@pequityresearch：摩根士丹利 Shawn Kim 将 SK 海力士 2029–2035 年平均 DRAM 营业利润率从 60% 下调至约 32%，并假设 HBM 相对标准 DRAM 的溢价将收窄（原文亦被截断）。@RealVision：外资七个交易日抛售超 12 万亿韩元韩股，SK 海力士周五一度 -10%，随后 KOSPI 收涨 4.39%，并称 HBM 大体已售至 2027。@grok 澄清：所谓 Sandisk 到 2028 年底回购 50% 股份，是摩根士丹利按近期价格、约营收 50% 节奏的预测，不是公司承诺；截至 10 月初剩余授权约 105 亿美元，公司可随时暂停。@WisemanCap：BofA 将 LRCX、MU、NVDA 移出 US 1 List，评级仍为买入。

含义：10 月晶圆厂和出口数据没有显示 AI 建设周期断裂；这与股价对 2027–28 年电力、许可和存储利润率的担忧可以同时成立。把 MS 的长期利润率下调、BofA 调出确信名单和韩股抛售写成“周期已经反转”，证据仍然不足——它们改变的是估值假设和风险偏好，不是已公布的砍单。3D DRAM 传闻若属实，长期影响的是逻辑代工与存储厂的边界，但目前只有媒体转述和台积电的口头划界。跟踪：11–12 月台积电是否好于季节性、Nvidia 11 月 17 日财报对 HBM 与库存的表述，以及海力士/美光对 2027 年后利润率的官方指引。

四、CoreWeave 交出增长与 backlog，但信用和电力的不对称没有消失

增量财务：@WisemanCap 记录 $CRWV 三季度 EPS（1.27 美元）好于预期（1.55 美元）（亏损收窄），营收 36.6 亿美元对预期 35.4 亿；2026 财年营收指引 130–133 亿美元，对预期 129 亿；截至 9 月 30 日收入 backlog 约 1325 亿美元。这是本期 neocloud 最硬的公司数字，来自账号对财报的摘录，请以公司原文为准。宏观上，同一账号记录 10 月 CPI 环比 0.4%（预期 0.3%）、核心 0.3%（预期 0.2%）——略热，可能影响对降息和数据中心融资成本的近端定价。

信用与电力（重叠时段，@nodiligence 的交易地图与公开报道，非官方统计）：以数据中心租约为支持的单 A 债券 8–10 月较国债 257bp（一季度 195bp）；hyperscaler 十年期利差今年走阔约 50bp；今年已发行约 2960 亿美元债券。作者认为债券市场看起来平静，是因为大部分建设还在“建成再换债券”的短期银行贷款里，Jupiter 贷款据报报价 89–91 美分，Anthropic 德州项目对应债券被搁置——这是日期风险，不是已宣布的违约。电力端：亚马逊、谷歌与核电的 15–20 年合约，对比 Meta 服务器 5.5 年折旧，说明需求若降温，更安全的现金流在发电商（如 $CEG）而非芯片或 GPU 云。软银 CFO 后藤芳光在电话会上否认马斯克“轮机订单违约”的指控，并与 $GE 管理层“未见取消”的说法对照——这是公司否认，不是对交付节奏的新指引。@SouthernValue95 转述 AWS CEO Matt Garman：认为未来一二十年里太空数据中心在经济上可行的可能性极低，发射仍稀少且昂贵。

含义：CRWV 的营收、指引和 1325 亿 backlog 说明至少这一家 GPU 云在报表上仍能把需求写成数字；但这并不能自动否定 nodiligence 所描绘的 refinancing 约束——backlog 不是现金，仍取决于电力、许可和把一年期建造贷款接成长期债券的能力。CPI 略高强化了“融资不会变得更便宜”的近端背景。对组合的含义仍是：已锁定长电价的发电商和能在自身资产负债表上等待的 hyperscaler，相对更依赖过桥和项目抵押的开发商/neocloud，不对称没有收窄。不要把单季 beats 外推成整个 AI 信用链已经畅通。

五、企业入口、安全与出口管制：平台层在抢控制平面，政策仍是“据报在推进”而非已立法

企业软件与分发（增量）：@OpenRouter 宣布正式成为 Stripe 的一部分，称超过 1000 万开发者和公司使用其服务，将保持模型与提供方中立，强调支出控制和数据策略。这是已宣布的交易，具体对价未在帖中给出。@levie：Box 与 Anthropic 合作，经 MCP 为 Claude 提供 Box 内容的完整读写互操作（创建/编辑合同、演示文稿等）。@ClaudeDevs：Claude 扩大对 MCP Apps 的支持，可在对话里运行完整应用；并向 Pro/Max/Ultra/Team 用户提供一次免费额度重置（12 月 10 日太平洋时间下午 2 点前）。@BenBajarin 把 Claude 进攻 Office、以及 OpenAI 预计会跟进，概括为“平台之战而非模型之战”，并称其 CISO/CSO 调研显示安全是企业 AI 预算能否落地的大门。@RihardJarc 指出 Meta 模型已可经 Google Cloud 使用，认为企业模型消费越来越集中在 Bedrock / Foundry / Vertex。@gdb 转发 OpenAI“在 ChatGPT 上认领企业”的产品入口。

安全事件与出口管制：@steph_palazzolo 称 Notion 也受到 Fireworks 黑客事件影响，并且“看来客户数据受到了波及”（不同于 Harvey 的情况）——这是记者转述，尚未见官方确认，不能写成已核实的数据泄露结论。出口管制主线仍主要在重叠时段：@jukan05 引 The Information，称黄仁勋事件后 Nvidia 游说效力下降，MATCH / Overwatch / Chip Security 三份法案更易进入国防授权法，白宫据报还要求加入阻断特定芯片远程访问的条款。增量：@AndrewCurran_ 称众议院外交事务委员会共和党主席对 ABC News 表示，若黄仁勋不在周三前回应闭门简报请求，准备发出传票；Nvidia 则声明已聘请 Joon H. Kim 及 Wachtell Lipton 进行调查。这些是程序进展，不是已经通过的法律或已经落实的远程访问禁令。

含义：模型价差之外，真正能改变收入归属的是谁控制企业工作流和数据平面——Stripe+OpenRouter 把多模型路由放进支付/账单基础设施，Box+Claude 把文件写权限关进一家模型供应商的界面，Office 入口则直接威胁微软的控制平面。这对 MSFT（被进攻）、CRM/办公套件、以及中立路由层都有份额含义，但不能从一则产品公告外推收入。政策上，传票威胁和内部调查增加了 NVDA 的治理与立法不确定性，但在 NDAA 文本和商务部规则出来之前，不宜把“中国相关需求已被切掉”当成基本面事实。Fireworks/Notion 若被证实影响客户数据，会强化 Ben Bajarin 所说的安全门，对独立推理云是近端信任冲击。

Highlights / Top 10｜按投资重要性排序

#1 ★@sundarpichai · 2026-11-11 00:04 北京时间

#7 @jukan05 · 2026-11-10 08:51 北京时间

要点：据 The Information，NVIDIA 长期以来大力游说，反对限制中国公司通过海外云服务商获取其最新芯片的提案。但自从黄仁勋丑闻爆发后，这种游说在华盛顿的分量似乎下降了。

余波据报使三份法案更容易推进。MATCH Act 将限制光刻设备对华出货。Overwatch Act 将加强国会对商务部批准向中国等国销售 AI 芯片的监督。Chip Security Act 将要求 AI 芯片具备内置追踪能力。

国防授权法预计将在新一届民主党主导的国会于 1 月开会之前通过。这给了共和党领导层现在就推动立法的动力，好在他们对最终文本还有较大发言权。

为何重要：黄仁勋事件后 Nvidia 游说效力据报下降，三份出口/监督/芯片追踪法案更易进入 NDAA；若再叠加远程访问限制，影响的是对华及海外云需求与 GPU 现货，而非一季晶圆厂出货。仍属立法进展转述，不是已通过的法律。

#10 ★@levie · 2026-11-11 05:04 北京时间

要点：代理未来与企业文件打交道的次数，将是今天人类的 100 倍。正因如此，我们很高兴与 Anthropic 合作，通过 Claude 为 Box 内容带来完整的读写互操作。

我们创办 Box 时，无法想象未来会以所有这些方式处理非结构化数据，但很清楚的是，代理和人都将产出远超我们想象的工件。我们正在打造的平台，让你可以极为顺畅且安全地，从任何代理处理你的内容。

为何重要：Box 与 Anthropic 经 MCP 打通企业文件的完整读写，是“平台之战而非模型之战”的具体合同：代理开始直接改写企业内容库。对 BOX、ANT 以及微软 Office 控制平面都有近端产品含义。

@nodiligence（保留 6 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 10 条）

2026-11-10 14:18 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

$NBIS $CRWV $META $GOOG

今年进入 AI 债务的每一条路径，贷款人都变得更挑剔；我认为感受最深的是 GPU 云，因为每建一座新集群都需要贷款人点头。

价格怎么动的：

—— 以数据中心租约为支持的单 A 债券，8–10 月较国债利差 257bp，一季度是 195bp。每 10 亿美元债务，一年大约多付 620 万美元。

—— 重复发行人 Switch 从 3 月的 225bp 升到 10 月的 290bp，所以不能把变化全解释成“上市的是哪些交易变了”。

—— hyperscaler 十年期利差今年走阔约 50bp，优质工业公司大约只走阔 20bp。他们今年已经发行约 2960 亿美元债券，超过高盛原先 2500 亿美元的估计，高盛预计 2027 年超过 4000 亿美元。

—— 摩根大通对“产生收入的不动产”（其备案称包括数据中心）的敞口，9 月末达到 294 亿美元，占其房地产敞口的 12.6%，三年前是 8%。

hyperscaler 也要付得更多，但背后是整个公司在撑。Nebius 9 月看过高收益债，10 月放弃，转借花旗 35 亿美元过桥，每 90 天更贵一次，直到 2027 年 7 月。

以我近来看到的情况，这对任何出租 GPU 的人来说，就是未来一年的样子：有担保、期限短、更贵。

原文：$NBIS $CRWV $META $GOOG

lenders got pickier on every route into AI debt this year, and i think the companies that feel it most are the GPU clouds, because they need a lender's yes for every new cluster.

where the price moved:

-- single-A bonds backed by data-centre leases paid 257bp over treasuries from august to october, against 195bp in the first quarter. on $1bn of debt that's about $6.2m a year more.

-- switch, a repeat borrower, went from 225bp in march to 290bp in october, so a change in which deals came to market can't explain all of it.

-- hyperscaler 10-year spreads widened about 50bp this year against about 20bp for strong industrial companies. they've already sold about $296bn of bonds this year, past goldman's $250bn estimate, and goldman sees more than $400bn in 2027.

-- jpmorgan's exposure to income-producing property, a category its filing says includes data centres, reached $29.4bn at the end of september, 12.6% of its real estate exposure against 8% three years earlier.

the hyperscalers pay more too, but the whole company stands behind them. nebius looked at a high-yield bond in september, dropped it in october and took a $3.5bn citi bridge that gets more expensive every 90 days until july 2027.

from what i'm seeing lately, that's what the next year looks like for anyone renting out GPUs: secured, short-dated and more expensive.

$NBIS $CRWV $C $GS

我每天都在更新这张 AI / 算力 / 宏观地图。

—— 以数据中心租约为支持的单 A 债券，8–10 月较国债 257bp，一季度 195bp。重复发行人 Switch 从 225bp 升至 290bp，交易结构变化解释不了全部走阔。

—— hyperscaler 十年期利差今年走阔约 50bp，对比优质工业约 20bp。今年已发行约 2960 亿美元债券，超过高盛 2500 亿估计，高盛看 2027 年超过 4000 亿。背后是整个公司在撑，所以我觉得 neocloud 会先感受到压力。

—— Nebius 放弃计划中的高收益债，转借花旗 35 亿美元过桥，利率 SOFR+2.75%，每 90 天加 0.25%，到 2027 年 7 月为 3.5%。若全额支取，最后一档比第一档一年多付 2625 万美元，整笔贷款 2027 年 10 月 16 日到期。

—— CoreWeave 的 GPU 贷款可支取 capex 的 71.42%。按一台 56.5 万美元的 B300 服务器、对照 H100 真实二手出售节奏，在任何还款之前垫子大概只够 24 到 32 个月，所以我认为风险更多在客户合同，而不是芯片本身。

—— 沙特基础设施基金与 Humain 签署 25 亿里亚尔贷款：11 亿里亚尔用于约 25MW 的 GPU，14 亿里亚尔用于按最高 1GW 设计的电网。Humain 为 PIF 51%、阿美 49%，我的猜测是风险留在沙特国家，关键在其余 975MW 填得有多慢。

原文：DAY 53:

$NBIS $CRWV $C $GS

i've been running this AI / compute / macro map every day: https://t.co/iDqZA9nyrN

-- single-A bonds backed by data-centre leases paid 257bp over treasuries from august to october against 195bp in the first quarter. switch, a repeat borrower, went from 225bp to 290bp, so the mix of deals can't explain all of it

-- hyperscaler 10-year spreads widened about 50bp this year against about 20bp for strong industrial companies. they've already sold about $296bn of bonds this year, past goldman's $250bn estimate, and goldman sees more than $400bn in 2027. the whole company stands behind them, so i think the neoclouds feel it first

-- nebius dropped a planned high-yield bond and took a $3.5bn citi bridge at SOFR + 2.75% that rises 0.25% every 90 days to 3.5% in july 2027. fully drawn, the last step costs $26.25m a year more than the first, and the whole loan matures on 16 october 2027

-- coreweave's GPU loan advances 71.42% of capex. on a $565,000 B300 server, the H100's real resale pace says the cushion lasts about 24 to 32 months before any repayment, so i think the customer contract carries more of the risk than the chips

-- saudi arabia's infra fund signed up to SAR 2.5bn of loans with humain: SAR 1.1bn for about 25MW of GPUs and SAR 1.4bn for grid designed for up to 1GW. humain is 51% PIF and 49% aramco, so my guess is the risk stays with the saudi state, and it comes down to how slowly the other 975MW fills

2026-11-10 19:32 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

$CEG

眼下 AI 里最安全的钱是电力合同，Constellation 这一季显示这种不对称已经有多明显。

hyperscaler 签下的电，会在芯片已经折旧完很久之后还在付：
—— 亚马逊：Calvert Cliffs 690MW、20 年
—— 谷歌：11 座核电机组 uprate（从现有反应堆挤出的额外出力）签 20 年，外加 2700MW、15 年

Meta 的服务器按 5.5 年折旧。2026 年买的一台服务器大约 2032 年就从账上消失，而把它启动的电力合同会跑到 2046 年。

这些 uprate 还可获得能源部支持、最高 50 亿美元、30 年、国债+0.375% 的贷款。

如果 AI 需求降温，Constellation 继续收款，hyperscaler 则要为不再运行的芯片承担 14 年电力。

原文：$CEG

the safest money in AI right now is the power contract, and constellation's quarter shows how lopsided it's got.

the hyperscalers are signing for electricity they'll still be paying for long after the chips are written off:
-- amazon: 690MW from calvert cliffs for 20 years
-- google: 20 years on the uprates (extra output squeezed out of existing reactors) at 11 nuclear units, plus 2,700MW for 15 years

meta writes its servers off over 5.5 years. so a server bought in 2026 is off the books by about 2032, and the power deal that switched it on runs to 2046.

and those uprates are getting a DOE-backed loan of up to $5bn for 30 years at 0.375% over treasuries.

if AI demand cools, constellation keeps getting paid and the hyperscaler carries 14 years of power it bought for chips it no longer runs.

2026-11-10 19:33 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

[X Article] The risk in AI data-centre debt is a refinancing date nobody can see
［X Article：《AI 数据中心债务的风险，是一个谁也看不见的再融资日期》长文要点，非逐字全文］债券市场看起来仍平静，作者认为是因为大部分 AI 建设还没进入债券，而在“建成后再换成债券”的短期银行贷款里。hyperscaler 可以发股或借 30 年，项目公司大约只借一年、必须有人在之后接走。

11 月 6 日美国投资级利差收于 0.93%、高收益 3.42%。hyperscaler 十年期今年走阔约 50bp（优质工业约 20bp）；5 年期单 A 数据中心债 8–10 月较国债 257bp（一季度 195bp）。Oracle 11 月 2 日新债票息 7%–8.65%。近两个月三笔最大建设贷款“卡住”：Jupiter 贷款据英国《金融时报》报价 89–91 美分（银行转售不出去）；Anthropic 德州 156 亿美元过桥对应的 50–70 亿美元债券被搁置；摩根大通与三菱日联为两个 Oracle 相关项目安排 380 亿美元建设贷花费数月。

在一座楼有电、有租户之前，外部看不到已提款多少、贷款值多少、由谁持有，债券只给已经建成并卖掉的部分定价。真正的风险是退出日期：电力和许可不断把日期往后推。作者自 9 月起手工追踪，称现有 74 国 1793 个站点、956 笔融资。美元贷款中 115 笔、合计 2960 亿美元已签署/关闭但无公开提款额，债券和 ABS 有 1750 亿美元现金可见。KKR 估计公募投资级买家大约只能覆盖 AI 建设 1.7 万亿美元，其余约 6.3 万亿（约 79%）要来自私募债务、资产支持和基础设施债。

Nexus（Anthropic 德州园区）是 142 亿美元一年期贷款、七家银行约各 20 亿；计划用 9 月高收益债偿还一部分。谷歌只在数据中心建成后才为 Anthropic 的租金和电费背书。德州在 ERCOT 完成全州审计前暂停数据中心许可，审计报告拟于 12 月 10 日提交监管。9 月 18–28 日：Jupiter 贷款折价、德州暂停许可、Oracle 向 Blue Owl 开发单元发不可抗力通知（租金或可推迟最多三年）、Nexus 债券搁置。作者强调这不是违约，而是日期风险；贷款人按 Oracle 评级给规模，但不可抗力把“以为是 Oracle 的风险”送回贷款人。

Alphabet 三季度筹资约 410 亿美元，其中 124 亿为新股、未回购；Meta 10 月 26 日定价约 375 亿美元新股本；Oracle 至 8 月当季 ATM 出售 200 亿美元股票，11 月 2 日再各 100 亿股票和债券。微软 9 月末有 3646 亿美元尚未起租的数据中心租赁，部分要到 2027–2034 财年、且须满足合同条件才开始。彭博 11 月 6 日称，在德州、俄亥俄、佐治亚、密歇根和新墨西哥，竞选时主张停止或暂停数据中心建设的州长当选，美国待建容量约 40% 位于这些州。

作者认为卡住的贷款主要落在日资和法资“持有型”银行（三菱日联同时出现在 Jupiter、Oracle-Vantage 和 Nexus）；风险转移（SRT）的买方往往已持有同一资产的股权，风险并未分散、只是更不透明。未来 90 天若出现：日法银行披露的数据中心提款敞口其实很小、Nexus 债券按时定价、或德州/新墨西哥给出明确许可日期，他会修正判断。作者声明为独立研究，并非投资建议。

原文：credit markets still look calm about AI, probably because most of the build isn’t in them yet. it’s in short bank loans waiting for a bond to pay them off, and power and permits keep pushing that back. three of the biggest are already stuck with banks. https://x.com/i/article/2120097814051168256

$ANT $GOOGL $NVDA $AMD $MSFT $AVGO $MU $TSM $AMZN

Anthropic 把 IPO 价格砍了一半，而且一部分对价来自它采购算力的那些公司。如果你第一天就进场，应该再想一想价格会怎么走。

七家供应商按 IPO 价认购 260 亿美元股票，约占 Anthropic 新发行股份的 30%。

—— 谷歌和 Nvidia 各投 100 亿美元，
—— AMD 25 亿美元，
—— Broadcom、美光和微软各 10 亿美元，
—— 台积电 5 亿美元。

再看看它们对 Anthropic 意味着什么。

—— 谷歌在 2033 年前至少有 1213 亿美元云容量应收（不论是否使用），Anthropic 还预期 2028 年起再向其采购 1250–1500 亿美元 TPU。
—— Broadcom 投了 10 亿，同时对租给 Anthropic 的设备有 1612 亿美元租金应收。
—— 微软在 2033 年前至少有 472 亿美元应收。AMD 的 25 亿计入它在一笔硬件协议里承诺的 50 亿美元股票，而 Anthropic 预期该协议下支出超过 200 亿美元，所以买股票本身就是芯片销售的一部分。
—— 亚马逊至少有 1100 亿美元应收，却完全没参与。它大概也不需要：4 月协议已允许 Anthropic 在新算力上线时，按 30 日均价再向其出售最多 150 亿美元股票。

这就是为什么估值砍半也没挡住它们。10 月初投资者把 Anthropic 看到最高 2 万亿美元。今天给出的区间，按 74 美元中位价约 9680 亿美元（若计入全部期权和授予约 1.33 万亿），预计下周开始交易。供应商仍按更低价格买，是因为它们的钱从来不是从股票里来的。对其他所有人来说，这次下调多半只是噪音。

5 月那一轮是 9650 亿美元，所以真正付过钱的人并没有亏，只有写过 2 万亿的人在账面上“亏了”。

对供应商这是一张廉价门票。260 亿美元不足 Anthropic 已承诺支出 9009 亿美元的 3%，而且它们的股份不在承销商锁定期内（出售仍需注册）。

如果 ANT 跌破 74 美元，谷歌和 Nvidia 在持股上会有一点浮亏，但合同还在收款。旁边的公开发行买方手里只有股票。

原文：$ANT $GOOGL $NVDA $AMD $MSFT $AVGO $MU $TSM $AMZN

anthropic halved its IPO price and is also partly paid for by the companies it buys compute from, and if you are going in at day 1, you should think twice what the price action would be.

seven suppliers are taking $26bn of stock at the IPO price, about 30% of the new shares anthropic sells.

-- google and nvidia put in $10bn each,
-- AMD $2.5bn,
-- broadcom, micron and microsoft $1bn each,
-- TSMC $500m.

and look who they are to anthropic.

-- google is owed at least $121.3bn for cloud capacity through 2033, used or not, and anthropic expects to pay it another $125bn to $150bn for TPUs from 2028.
-- broadcom put in $1bn and is owed $161.2bn of rent on the equipment it leases anthropic.
-- microsoft is owed at least $47.2bn through 2033. AMD's $2.5bn counts toward the $5bn of stock it promised inside a hardware deal anthropic expects to spend over $20bn on, so the stock buy is part of the chip sale.
-- amazon is owed at least $110bn and isn't in it at all. it probably doesn't need to be. its april deal already lets anthropic sell it up to $15bn more stock later, at the 30-day average price, as new compute comes online.

which is why the valuation getting halved hasn't put any of them off. in early october investors were putting anthropic at up to $2tn. the range it set today comes to about $968bn at the $74 midpoint (about $1.33tn if you count every option and grant), and it's expected to start trading next week. the suppliers are still buying at the lower price because the stock was never where their money comes from. for everyone else the cut is mostly noise btw.

the may round was $965bn, so nobody who actually paid is down, only the people who wrote $2tn.

for the suppliers this is a cheap ticket. $26bn is under 3% of the $900.9bn anthropic has committed to spend, and their shares aren't in the underwriters' lockup (they still need registration to sell).

if ANT trades under $74, google and nvidia take a small hit on the stake and keep collecting on the contracts. the public buyer next to them only has the stock.

2026-11-10 18:43 北京时间 ｜ Source: @nodiligence ｜ 原帖链接

沙特国家基础设施基金 11 月 8 日与 Humain 签署 25 亿里亚尔（约 6.67 亿美元）贷款，我的猜测是国家在租户到位之前就先为电网放贷。

—— 最多 11 亿里亚尔（约 2.93 亿美元）用于约 25MW IT 负载的 GPU。

—— 最多 14 亿里亚尔（约 3.73 亿美元）用于按最高 1GW 设计的变电站与并网。

—— 利率、期限、担保和租户均未披露。

Humain 自 11 月 1 日起为 PIF 51%、阿美 49%，阿美 2026 年将注入约 99 亿里亚尔（约 26 亿美元）现金，大约是两笔贷款合计的四倍。所以我认为 Humain 大部分芯片自己出资，基础设施基金的钱真正用在电网上。

利率未披露，下限是三个月 SAIBOR（8 月 4.83%，9 月 5.01%–5.54%）。14 亿里亚尔全额的利息一年约 6800 万至 7800 万里亚尔（1800–2100 万美元），按 25MW 计每 MWh IT 容量约 82–94 美元，按满负荷 1GW 则约 2 美元。

挂牌 3.90 美元/小时的 H200，每 MWh 约赚 3060 美元；按已备案一年期交易均价 1.67 美元则约 1310 美元，所以即便只有 25MW，利息也只占租金的 3%–7%。

真正的风险是其余 975MW 填租有多慢；国家既是股东又是贷款人，这笔风险留在国家。

原文：saudi arabia's national infrastructure fund signed up to SAR 2.5bn ($667m) of loans with humain on 8 november, and my guess is the state is lending for grid well ahead of its tenants.

-- up to SAR 1.1bn ($293m) for GPUs for about 25MW of IT load.

-- up to SAR 1.4bn ($373m) for substations and grid links designed for up to 1GW.

-- no rate, tenor, security or tenant disclosed.

humain has been 51% PIF and 49% aramco since 1 november, and aramco is contributing about SAR 9.9bn ($2.6bn) in cash during 2026, roughly four times both loans together. so i think humain funds most of the chips itself and infra's money is really about the grid.

with no rate disclosed, the floor is three-month SAIBOR, 4.83% in august and 5.01% to 5.54% in september. interest alone on the full SAR 1.4bn is SAR 68m to 78m ($18m to $21m) a year, about $82 to $94 per MWh of IT capacity at 25MW and about $2 at the full 1GW.

H200s listed at $3.90 an hour earn about $3,060 per MWh, or about $1,310 at the $1.67 filed one-year deals averaged, so even at 25MW the interest is 3% to 7% of the rent.

the genuine risk here is how slowly the other 975MW would fill, and with the state as owner and lender, that risk stays with the state.

有的，我依然关注追踪Innodata $INOD. 这期财报我也看了。@Ycwong603415551

我有一种感觉，就是我越研究，越感觉我对Innodata的把握性在降低。

这种迷茫感来自于：

1）新CEO上任之后，这次财报新提出了六大战略方向；这和一年前的战略方向还是有很大差距的。当然，主因还是AI发展速度日新月异，因此Innodata的技术方向转向是可以理解的。但严格来讲，宣布进军/押注热门领域是一回事，能否成功又是一回事。比如说第一个方向就是具身智能，这当然听起来就是高大上。但是据我所知，为具身智能提供数据服务的公司可是不少，INOD能否脱颖而出，难说。

CEO也说了，27年重点是看执行。

2）信息沟通还是不够（至少按我的标准来看）。我理解这当然是有部分原因在于为客户保密。但是我关注的半导体领域其他公司（比如 $AEHR, $VICR），也面临客户保密的前提，我至少感觉, 我越研究越明白；但Innodata给我的感觉不是这样，让我有种挫败感。

这次财报的问答部分，我对CEO和CFO的评分也不高。我感觉这两位对于分析师的问题（以及潜在问题）都没有回答到点上，或者是官方回答，或者是浅尝辄止，或者是直接生硬拒绝了。

3）财报结果：猛一看营收/利润都超越了市场预期，也提升了全年业绩指引。但是市场/股票走势反应不佳。除了外部环境的影响之外，我认为的主要槽点在于

--曾经的最大客户因某个项目的终止，在下季度将导致公司带来1200万的损失。当然公司也安慰说，预计该客户随着现有项目的进展，在27Q1 带来的营收会持续增长，覆盖这损失。

我有些迷惑，这1200万美元的损失具体是指什么。感觉好像是原先某个项目提前终止了，因此营收比预期的会减少1200万？

--之前因为印度分公司大裁员传闻导致公司股价暴跌，这次分析师没有直接询问，但是选择曲线发问（不同项目人员是否可以复用等）。管理层的回答看上去是一个标准答案，说要看项目性质而定。但实际效果我感觉不好，因为数据工程这行业，大家都瞧不上低端的人工标注/数据标注，连带着瞧不上项目制，因为那样无法复用，利润低。之前财报管理层也说过要尽量通过平台方式复用，以提升利润率。但这次的回答，会让人感觉还是“低端”

总之，Innodata给我现在的感觉是：身处最热门的领域AI，然后追求AI最热门的细分领域（比如具身智能，智能体，安全等等），热门名词是都全了。

然后潜在客户自然也都是高大上，Mag7，前沿实验室，Hyperscaler等等。但这里有两点，就是这些著名客户有多少是其AI领域的大客户是值得深思的（很多客户在互联网时代和Innodata合作过），其次是合作伙伴（这次列出了Anthropic这些名字）并不意味着是其AI领域的大客户。

公司确实有很大的机会，前景确实广阔。之前的股价上涨已经反映了这点。

但投资者的要求也在升高，不仅要求AI营收要增长，更要求盈利质量。我认为这点目前公司还没做到。

@buccocapital（保留 1 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 3 条）

2026-11-11 01:32 北京时间 ｜ Source: @buccocapital ｜ 原帖链接

我不会这样概括企业 AI 采用的现状。广义上，衡量一个组织里 AI 的 ROI 有两个变量：
1. 你在自动化或增强的是什么任务
2. 这家企业到底清不清楚自己为什么能成功？

#1 任务。
举个很简单的例子：关闭一张客户支持工单。大多数公司按渠道知道这要花多少钱——比如电话 40 美元、邮件 15 美元、聊天 5 美元。这是用坐席薪资、支持他们的编制、所用技术、场地，以及差旅和培训费用算出来的。

现在想象你能用 AI *成功地*回答一个问题，而你用的那家 AI 公司为此收 70 美分。好，在这个具体、边界清晰的用例里，你非常清楚 AI 的 ROI。

再看其他任务。你推出新产品的速度。更模糊一些，但要判断 1）开发者生产力是否提高、2）这是否把收入曲线的斜率往上掰，也并非离谱的任务。

再往曲线远端走。现在能用品牌设计资产生成 21 个不同版本的营销创意；让 40% 的分析请求变成自助，而不再卡在等人回答的队列里。要直接测量 ROI，就混乱得多、更难直接衡量。

#2 公司是否把事理顺了？

他们是否知道到底是什么在推动业务向前？我认为很多企业的答案其实是：不知道。他们是否可靠地知道谁是顶尖贡献者？是否知道真正驱动收入增长的那两三个输入？不仅知道，能否加快它们？如果你问公司里 10 个人“对公司最重要的事是什么”，他们会给出同一个答案吗？同样，我觉得大多是否定的。

如果你不能深入理解自己的业务、它的驱动力、以及谁在驱动它，就很难知道 AI 是否在帮你。
----------
TL;DR：有些任务很容易衡量 ROI，有些则不然。有些把事理顺了的企业很容易衡量 ROI，有些则不然。

（回复/引用 @TheStalwart 关于“不知道这些支出有没有回报，但员工会因为工具被拿走而崩溃”的概括）

原文：I wouldn't characterize the state of enterprise AI adoption this way. Broadly, I would say there are two variables in measuring the ROI of AI in an organization:
1. The task you are automating or augmenting
2. Does this business have any fucking idea why they are successful?

#1 The task.
Let's take a very simple example. Closing out a support ticket from a customer. Most companies know how much this costs by channel - say, $40 to pick up the phone, $15 to answer it by email, $5 by chat. This is calculating using things like the agent salaries, the headcount that supports them, the technology they use, the facilities, and any travel and training expenses

Now imagine you can use AI to *successfully* answer a question and the AI company you use charges you 70 cents for that. OK, you really clearly know the ROI of AI in this specific, bounded, use case.

Consider other tasks. The velocity with which you ship new products. A bit fuzzier but not a crazy task to determine whether 1) Developer productivity has increased 2) Whether that is bending the slope of your revenue curve upward.

Farther out on the curve. Being able to now generate 21 different versions of a marketing campaign's creative using your brand design assets. Making 40% of analytics requests self-service instead of being stuck in a queue for humans to answer. Quite a bit messier to measure direct ROI.

#2 Does the company have it's shit together?

Do they know what is actually driving the business forward? I think that the answer for many businesses is actually, No. Do they reliably know who their top performers are? Do they know the actual 2-3 inputs that drive revenue growth? Not only do they know them but can they accelerate them? If you asked 10 people at the company what "the most important thing" was for the business, would they give the same answer? Again, I think the answer is mostly No.

If you do not deeply understand your business, what is driving it, and who is driving it, it is quite hard to know whether AI is helping you.
----------
TL;DR. For some tasks it is quite easy to measure the ROI. For others, less so. For some businesses who have their shit together, it is quite easy to measure the ROI. For others, less so.

引用原帖（@TheStalwart）：Would it be fair to characterize the state of enterprise AI adoption as:

"We don't really know if we're getting any return from all this spending, but our employees would lose their shit if we took the tools away?" 链接

2026-11-10 13:28 北京时间 ｜ Source: @SemiAnalysis_ ｜ 原帖链接

🚨 HBM5 在不增加任何引脚的情况下把带宽翻倍 🚨

HBM5 保持与 HBM4E 相同的 2048 个引脚，但把每个引脚跑得快了将近 2 倍。三星在 Hot Chips 2026 的报告显示，引脚速率从 HBM4E 的 14 Gbps 提升到 HBM5 的超过 28 Gbps。

更快的引脚只是故事的一半。为了喂饱每个引脚，引脚背后的一切也要加倍：DRAM bank、TSV，以及基片上升级的 PHY。
下面用超市来解释这是怎么工作的 🛒（1/6）

原文：🚨 HBM5 Doubles Bandwidth Without Adding a Single Pin 🚨

HBM5 keeps the same 2048 pins as HBM4E and runs each one nearly 2× faster. Samsung's Hot Chips 2026 presentation shows pin speed going from 14 Gbps on HBM4E to over 28 Gbps on HBM5.

Faster pins are only half the story. To keep each pin fed, everything behind the pins doubles too: DRAM banks, TSVs, and an upgraded PHY on the base die.
Here's how it works, explained as a grocery store 🛒 (1/6)🧵

2026-11-11 03:36 北京时间 ｜ Source: @PhotonCap ｜ 原帖链接

- Xanadu（$XNDU）与 HyperLight 于 11 月 10 日宣布，TFLN 光开关的插入损耗为 7 mdB。

- 以 dB 计的损耗较 2025 年宣布的约 20 mdB 基准下降了 65%。

- 把所报告数字换成光子损耗，大约是每次开关通过 0.46% 对 0.16%。

- 两家公司称，在 HyperLight 的 TFLN Chiplet 规模晶圆制造线上实现了高良率和可重复性。

- 新闻稿未给出数值良率、样本量、开关速度、消光比，以及封装损耗是否计入。

- TFLN 是薄膜铌酸锂，利用普克尔效应通过电场改变折射率，以实现快速光学控制。

- Xanadu 另外报告，光纤到芯片耦合损耗平均为每端面 0.085 dB。

- HyperLight 在 2026 年 3 月宣布与 $UMC 和 Wavetek 合作，进行 6 英寸和 8 英寸 TFLN 代工生产。

> 量子信息无法通过传统光放大从损耗中恢复，所以更低的开关损耗直接保全到达计算的光子。

> 仅假设 100 次相同的开关通过，计算得到的透过率从 63.1% 升至 85.1%；该示意未计入实际系统的路由深度和所有其他损耗。

> 同样的开关损耗预算大约能容纳 2.9 倍的通过次数，但这并不意味着逻辑量子比特或计算性能增加 2.9 倍。

> 单独报告的每端面 85 mdB 耦合损耗，大约相当于新开关的 12 次通过，因此封装和芯片间连接仍是降低总光路损耗的核心。

> 在 AI、电信和量子计算之间共享 TFLN 制造基础设施，可能分摊工艺开发和设备成本，不过针对量子级损耗要求的良率需要单独验证。

> 直接的技术好处归于 $XNDU 的光路设计和 HyperLight 的平台，而 $UMC 的收入敞口取决于实际产能分配和产量。

> 这颗 7 mdB 器件的制造地点未披露，因此现有代工合作并不构成对该器件的确认订单。

> 投资者下一步应跟踪封装后的总损耗、目标速度下的消光比、晶圆到晶圆的波动，以及逻辑错误率的改善。

> 数据中心被列为潜在应用；公告中既没有 AI 客户订单，也没有商业系统出货时间表。

原文：- Xanadu ($XNDU) and HyperLight announced 7 mdB insertion loss for a TFLN optical switch on November 10.

- Loss measured in dB fell 65% from the approximately 20 mdB benchmark announced in 2025.

- Converting the reported figures into photon loss gives approximately 0.46% versus 0.16% per switch pass.

- The companies reported high yield and repeatability on HyperLight’s TFLN Chiplet volume wafer manufacturing line.

- The release omitted numerical yield, sample size, switching speed, extinction ratio, and whether packaging loss was included.

- TFLN is thin-film lithium niobate, which uses the Pockels effect to change refractive index with an electric field for fast optical control.

- Xanadu separately reported average fiber-to-chip coupling loss of 0.085 dB per facet.

- HyperLight announced a partnership with $UMC and Wavetek for 6-inch and 8-inch TFLN foundry production in March 2026.

> Quantum information cannot recover from loss through conventional optical amplification, so lower switch loss directly preserves photons reaching the computation.

> Assuming only 100 identical switch passes, calculated transmission rises from 63.1% to 85.1%; this illustration excludes actual system routing depth and all other losses.

> The same switch loss budget could accommodate approximately 2.9 times as many passes, without implying a 2.9-fold increase in logical qubits or computational performance.

> The separately reported 85 mdB coupling loss per facet equals approximately 12 passes through the new switch, keeping packaging and inter-chip connections central to reducing total optical path loss.

> Sharing TFLN manufacturing infrastructure across AI, telecom, and quantum computing could spread process development and equipment costs, although yield against quantum-grade loss requirements needs separate validation.

> The direct technical benefits accrue to $XNDU’s optical path design and HyperLight’s platform, while revenue exposure for $UMC depends on actual production allocation and volume.

> The fabrication site for the 7 mdB device was undisclosed, so the existing foundry partnership does not establish a confirmed order for this device.

> Investors should next track total loss after packaging, extinction ratio at target speed, wafer-to-wafer variation, and improvements in logical error rates.

> Data centers were presented as a potential application; the announcement included neither AI customer orders nor a commercial system shipment schedule.

2026-11-11 04:12 北京时间 ｜ Source: @PhotonCap ｜ 原帖链接

- 北京大学与 SiFotonics 的研究者演示了一个统一的硅光相干平台，覆盖短距和长距光通信。

- 短距光学传统上依赖 VCSEL、EML 和硅光 IM-DD，长距网络则偏好相干传输。

- 研究者开发了一种高功率、窄线宽激光器，将超低损耗 SiN 外腔与 RSOA 结合。

- 该激光器实现了 70 mW 直接输出、亚 1 kHz 线宽，以及 13.7% 的壁插效率。

- 与硅光相干收发器结合，该平台在 256 个波长通道上演示了合计 81.92 Tbps 的带宽。

- 短距传输无需光放大，另一项长距演示超过 4400 公里。

- 波长切换与传输重建在数十微秒内完成。

> PhotonCap 要点

> 关键含义是把相干光学从长距网络延伸到 AI 数据中心。

> SiN 外腔的低相位噪声和高光输出，满足相干收发器的关键激光器要求。

> 短距相干的采用可能扩大 DSP、相干接收器、可调谐激光器和硅光 PIC 的机会。

> 所演示的 81.92 Tbps 是 256 个波长的合计带宽，不是单条光链路。

> 对 AI scale-up 而言，相干 DSP 的功耗、延迟和成本必须变得能与传统 IM-DD 竞争。

> $LITE 和 $COHR 拥有相关激光技术，$MRVL 有相干 DSP 敞口，但该研究并未确立直接的商业利益。

> 关键投资问题是：在哪些传输距离上，相干光学能在每比特能耗和每比特成本上胜过 IM-DD。

原文：- Peking University and SiFotonics researchers demonstrated a unified silicon photonic coherent platform spanning short- and long-distance optical communications.

- Short-reach optics traditionally rely on VCSEL, EML, and SiPh-based IM-DD, while long-haul networks favor coherent transmission.

- The researchers developed a high-power, narrow-linewidth laser combining an ultralow-loss SiN external cavity with an RSOA.

- The laser achieved 70 mW direct output, sub-1 kHz linewidth, and 13.7% wall-plug efficiency.

- Combined with SiPh coherent transceivers, the platform demonstrated 81.92 Tbps aggregate bandwidth across 256 wavelength channels.

- Short-reach transmission required no optical amplification, while a separate long-haul demonstration exceeded 4,400 km.

- Wavelength switching and transmission re-establishment were achieved within tens of microseconds.

> PhotonCap Takeaway

> The key implication is extending coherent optics beyond long-haul networks into AI data centers.

> Low phase noise and high optical output from SiN external cavities address critical laser requirements for coherent transceivers.

> Short-reach coherent adoption could expand opportunities for DSPs, coherent receivers, tunable lasers, and SiPh PICs.

> The demonstrated 81.92 Tbps represents aggregate bandwidth across 256 wavelengths, not a single optical link.

> For AI scale-up, coherent DSP power, latency, and cost must become competitive with conventional IM-DD.

> $LITE and $COHR have relevant laser technologies, while $MRVL has coherent DSP exposure, but the research does not establish direct commercial benefits.

> The critical investment question is where coherent optics can outperform IM-DD in energy per bit and cost per bit across transmission distances.

- 在测试的 8 个量子构建块中，Claude 在 7 个上降低了 Toffoli 门数量和量子比特需求，综合指标最高改进达 39 倍。

> 该研究表明，AI 辅助的算法设计可以降低量子电路的资源需求，有可能加快实用量子计算应用的时间表。
> 这也表明，在容错量子时代，AI 工具可能成为弥合硬件能力与算法效率差距的关键。

原文：- QuEra, Harvard, and Anthropic published research showing AI can design quantum circuits with fewer computational resources.
- Claude reduced Toffoli-gate counts and qubit requirements in 7 of 8 quantum building blocks tested, with up to 39x improvement in combined metrics.
- AI discovered a new reversible approach to modular inversion, offering new trade-offs between gate and qubit counts for elliptic-curve operations.

> The research demonstrates that AI-assisted algorithm design can reduce resource requirements for quantum circuits, potentially accelerating the timeline for practical quantum computing applications.
> This suggests AI tools may become essential for bridging the gap between hardware capabilities and algorithm efficiency in the fault-tolerant quantum era.

据 The Information，NVIDIA 长期以来大力游说，反对限制中国公司通过海外云服务商获取其最新芯片的提案。但自从黄仁勋丑闻爆发后，这种游说在华盛顿的分量似乎下降了。

余波据报使三份法案更容易推进。MATCH Act 将限制光刻设备对华出货。Overwatch Act 将加强国会对商务部批准向中国等国销售 AI 芯片的监督。Chip Security Act 将要求 AI 芯片具备内置追踪能力。

国防授权法预计将在新一届民主党主导的国会于 1 月开会之前通过。这给了共和党领导层现在就推动立法的动力，好在他们对最终文本还有较大发言权。

原文：According to The Information, NVIDIA has long lobbied hard against proposals to restrict Chinese companies’ access to its latest chips through overseas cloud providers. But since the scandal surrounding Jensen Huang broke, that lobbying appears to carry less weight in Washington.

The fallout has reportedly made it easier to advance three bills. The MATCH Act would restrict shipments of lithography equipment to China. The Overwatch Act would give Congress greater oversight of the Commerce Department’s decisions to approve AI chip sales to countries such as China. The Chip Security Act would require AI chips to have built-in tracking capabilities.

The National Defense Authorization Act is expected to pass before the new Democratic-led Congress convenes in January. That gives Republican leaders an incentive to help move the legislation forward now, while they still have more say over what goes into the final bill.

2026-11-10 21:27 北京时间 ｜ Source: @jukan05 ｜ 原帖链接

Anthropic 的 S-1 披露了其昂贵的短期芯片租赁（指向 SpaceX）

—— 大部分算力在 12 到 18 个月前通过多年期合同锁定。当客户或研究需求超过该容量时，公司会购买短期算力，目前其成本“显著高于”多年期合同下的可比算力。

—— 规模为三季度 50 亿美元，前九个月 65 亿美元。

—— 营业利润从二季度的 9.70 亿美元，转为三季度营业亏损 5.70 亿美元。公司将其归因于 50 亿美元短期算力以及 20 亿美元非现金捐赠匹配费用。

—— 短期算力的使用近期末还会继续增加，营业利润率也将因此受压。

—— 截至 9 月末，短期合同（一年内可无罚金取消，或剩余期限一年或以内）项下最低付款约 174 亿美元。10 月以来又新签约 66 亿美元。

—— 一旦 Anthropic Direct（公司直接租赁芯片并在专用数据中心托管）上线，公司将用更便宜的长期算力，替换需求高峰期购买的短期算力。

解读：

—— 剔除短期容量后，三季度算力成本为 48.6 亿美元，低于二季度合计的 55.6 亿美元。长期合约容量在三季度几乎没有增长。实际上，54 亿美元的收入增量是靠短期容量承接的。

—— 每美元算力对应的收入从二季度的 2.08 美元降至三季度的 1.72 美元。公司也指出该指标较前一季度下降。

—— 174 亿美元短期合同余额加上 66 亿美元新签约，合计 240 亿美元，约占未来 12 个月应付 707 亿美元的三分之一。已经签约的量超过了三季度 50 亿美元的季度节奏。

问题：2027 年 3 月之后，Anthropic 还会继续向 SpaceX 支付巨额溢价吗？

来源：Anthropic 的 S-1 备案

原文：What Anthropic's S-1 discloses about its expensive short term chip rentals (aimed at SpaceX)

- Most compute is secured 12 to 18 months in advance under multiyear contracts. When customer or research demand exceeds that capacity, the company buys short term compute, which "currently" costs "significantly more" than comparable compute under multiyear contracts.

- The scale was $5.0 billion in Q3 and $6.5 billion over the first nine months.

- Operating income of $970 million in Q2 turned into an operating loss of $570 million in Q3. The company attributes this to $5.0 billion of short term compute and $2.0 billion of noncash donation matching charges.

- Use of short term compute will keep increasing in the near term, and operating margin will be compressed as a result.

- As of the end of September, minimum payments under short term contracts (cancelable without penalty within one year, or with a remaining term of one year or less) were about $17.4 billion. About $6.6 billion more has been signed since October.

- Once Anthropic Direct, under which the company leases chips directly and hosts them in dedicated data centers, comes online, the company will replace the short term compute bought during demand surges with cheaper long term compute.

Interpretation:

- Excluding short term capacity, Q3 compute cost was $4.86 billion, below the Q2 total of $5.56 billion. Long term contracted capacity barely grew in Q3. In effect, the $5.4 billion increase in revenue was served with short term capacity.

- Revenue per dollar of compute fell from $2.08 in Q2 to $1.72 in Q3. The company also notes that this metric declined from the prior quarter.

- The $17.4 billion short term contract balance plus the $6.6 billion of new contracts comes to $24.0 billion, about one third of the $70.7 billion due over the next 12 months. More than the Q3 pace ($5.0 billion a quarter) is already under contract.

Question: Will Anthropic keep paying SpaceX a huge premium after March 2027?

Source: Anthropic’s S-1 filing

2026-11-11 04:24 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@tphuang ｜ 原帖链接

智元机器人发布了其机器人模型 WITA-Omni 的最新版本，声称在所有 Omni 排名体系上达到 SOTA。

最近我看到一些人讨论 Fable 和 Astra 在机器人上有多强。

通用大模型不适用于 AI 机器人，有很多显而易见的原因，但最明显的一条是：模型必须在端侧运行。

因此，你需要更小、聚焦于机器人绝对必需事项的模型，才能高效。它必须小到能放进板载内存。考虑到 KV Cache 的大小，模型规模比你想的要小得多。

通过 WiFi 去调用 Claude 不是选项：反复调用成本过高，再加上确实可能进入信号盲区。我理解 Elon 可能想用星链给 Optimus 机器人和 Grok 推理，但我可以向你保证，纽约有很多建筑里这行不通。

除此之外，专用模型还可以适配该机器人可用的传感器。例如，电子皮肤的特性会成为任何机器人应用的主要输入和训练数据。机器人做的很多事将由触觉传感器决定，而这又取决于手部和身体传感器的位置。这和用视觉传感器数据训练大模型并不一样。

原文：Agibot released latest version of its robotic model WITA-Omni, which it claims to be SOTA across all the Omni ranking systems.

Now, I've seen some ppl talking about how great Fable & Astra are for robotics recently.

There are many obvious reasons why general LLMs don't work for AI robots, but the most obvious one is that you need the model to run on edge.

As such, you need smaller model focused on the things that robot absolutely needs in order to be efficient. It needs to be small enough to be held on the on-board memory. Consider the size of KV Cache, the model sizes are a lot smaller than you'd think.

Wifi to Claude is not an option due to the excessive cost from calling it over and over again + the very real possibility of hitting dead zones. I understand that Elon may want to use Starlink for Optimus robots & Grok inference. But I can assure of many New York buildings where this would not work.

And aside from that, specialized models can cater to the sensors available to that robot. For example, the properties of electronic skin would be major input & training data for any robot applications. Much of what a robot does would be determined by touch sensors, which is again dependent on the hand sensor and body sensors locations. Not exactly the same as using vision sensor data to train your LLMs.

（animated_gif：仅预览图，完整内容见原帖）

@demian_ai（保留 2 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 9 条）

2026-11-11 05:32 北京时间 ｜ Source: @demian_ai ｜ 原帖链接

人们曾经说：“计算机时代到处都能看见，唯独看不见在生产率统计里。”

工厂用上电动机大约 40 年后，才真正变得更快。

第一台大约在 1884 年进入工厂。

到 1899 年，电动机还运转着美国工厂里不到 5% 的机器，直到大约 1919 年才超过一半。

即便那时，多数厂主做的仍是最显而易见的事：把地下室里的大型蒸汽机拆掉，在原来的位置放上一台大电动机，其他一切照旧。

一台电机带动天花板上的一根长铁轴，皮带从上面落到地板上的每台机器，整个系统从早转到晚，不管你开的是一台车床还是五十台。

好处是在人们停止复制旧布局之后才出现的。当每台机器有了自己的小电机，你不再需要那些轴，也就不再需要围着动力源堆起来的又高又窄的厂房。

工厂变成平坦的单层，机器按工序流动的顺序排列，一根皮带断裂也不再让整间屋子停工。

单位资本的动力在 30 年里几乎没变，然后在 1920 和 30 年代快速上升，也正是工厂生产率起飞的那些年。

斯坦福经济学家保罗·戴维在 1990 年用这个故事，回答罗伯特·索洛那句“计算机时代到处可见、生产率统计里却看不见”。

计算机带来的好处，几年后、在 90 年代末确实出现了。

所以当人们争论这类图表、争论 AI 是否已经开始兑现时，我会看谁在拆除那根天轴，也就是谁在推倒重来。

大多数公司仍只是把模型螺栓固定在旧的组织架构上，但我的猜测是：当有人围绕它建成“单层厂房”版的公司时，数字才会动起来。

（所引用 @GavinSBaker：认为 FT 的图表、尤其第一张，已能看到一些“AI 的 ROI”证据。）

原文：People used to say « you could see the computer age everywhere but in the productivity statistics »

Factories had electric motors for about 40 years before they got much faster

the first one went into a factory around 1884.

by 1899 motors ran less than 5% of the machinery in american factories, and they didn't pass half until about 1919.

even then most owners did the obvious thing. they pulled the big steam engine out of the basement, put a big electric motor where it used to sit, and kept everything else.

one motor turned a long iron shaft along the ceiling, belts dropped from it to every machine on the floor, and the whole thing spun from morning to night whether you were running one lathe or fifty.

the gains showed up once people stopped copying the old layout. when every machine got its own small motor, you didn't need the shafts, so you didn't need tall narrow buildings stacked around the power source.

Factories went flat and single story, machines got lined up in the order the work moved, and one snapped belt no longer stopped a whole room.

Power per unit of capital stayed flat for 30 years, then climbed fast through the 1920s and 30s, the same years factory productivity took off.

paul david, the stanford economist, used this in 1990 to answer robert solow's line that you could see the computer age everywhere but in the productivity statistics.

The computer gains did show up a few years later, in the late 90s.

So when people argue over charts like these about whether ai is paying off yet, i'd watch who tears out the line shaft aka rebuilds from scratch

most companies are still bolting a model onto the old org chart but my guess is the numbers move when somebody builds the single story version of a company around it

Seems like a productivity surge post-Covid era overhiring that began to reverse before ChatGPT came out and then resumed its upward trend after ChatGPT.

Especially given the @a16z data on revenue per employee at their portfolio companies and the @EPBResearch data showing that the… 链接

2026-11-10 23:27 北京时间 ｜ Source: @demian_ai ｜ 原帖链接

这很有意思。

在 1870 年代，一份中文电报比英文电报更贵，而今天的大模型对很多语言做了类似的事。

摩斯电码是围绕英语建的。“E”作为最常见字母，只是一个点。

中文有数千个字、没有字母表，所以 1871 年的中文电报码给每个字一个四位数，而每个数字要花五个点划——摩斯电码里最长的符号。一个字要发出二十个信号。

于是一条 25 个词的电报，中文编码大约要半小时，英文大约只要两分钟。

大模型按 token 计费，也就是分词器把文本切成的块。

分词器主要从英文和拉丁字母网页文本里学习切块，于是其他语言被切得更碎，要为更多块付钱。

即便在网上最大的 20 种语言里，最贵的也需要最便宜那种的 1.5 到 5 倍 token；在 200 种语言上，某一模型的差距达到 17 倍。

更多 token 还意味着上下文窗口能装下的更少，回答也更慢。文字系统本身比语言有多罕见伤害更大：缅甸文、藏文、高棉文和老挝文最吃亏。

每张网络都按建造者所用的单位定价，用其他单位写作的人就要一直付这个差价，直到有人重新设计这套编码。

原文：this is fascinating

in the 1870s a chinese telegram cost more than an english one, and LLMs do a version of the same thing to a lot of languages today

morse code was built around english.
"E", the most common letter, is a single dot.

Chinese has thousands of characters and no alphabet, so the 1871 chinese telegraph code gave each character a four digit number, and every digit costs five dots and dashes, the longest symbols in morse. One character went out as twenty signals.

-> so a 25 word message took about half an hour to encode, against about two minutes in english.

LLMs bill by the token, the chunks a tokenizer chops your text into.

Tokenizers learned their chunks mostly from english and latin script web text, so other languages get chopped finer and pay for more pieces.

even among the 20 biggest languages online, the priciest needs 1.5 to 5 times the tokens of the cheapest, and across 200 languages one model's gap reached 17x.

More tokens also means less fits in the context window and answers come back slower. The script hurts more than how rare the language is: burmese, tibetan, khmer and lao come off worst. ​

Every network gets priced in the units of whoever built it, and everyone writing in other units pays the difference until somebody redesigns the code.

📊 SMH: $487.41 (+5.05% vs prior trading close)

• Nvidia and Jensen Huang face a proposed shareholder class action alleging they concealed his ties to a suspected Chinese spy. The lawsuit adds securities-litigation exposure to the existing governance crisis. These are allegations, not findings; the investment risk is escalating legal and export-policy scrutiny.

• Google unveiled TPU 8tx and 8i Max—new systems built around existing eighth-generation silicon, not a new chip generation. 8tx pairs with AMD EPYC hosts for confidential computing; dual-chip 8i Max doubles compute and HBM bandwidth. Google now counts nearly 100 TPU customers, widening its accelerator challenge.

• Sanmina delivered $3.86B of fiscal Q4 revenue and $4.43 adjusted EPS, with a 9% adjusted operating margin. FY2027 guidance calls for $17.5–19.5B revenue and $15.50–17.50 adjusted EPS. ZT Systems integration strengthens its cloud/AI position, while the core business also grew double digits over the full year.

• SpaceX proposed roughly $24B of initial investment in a gas-fired power plant and supercomputing facility at Starbase, Texas. A December 9 hearing will consider tax abatements. Owning generation could bypass grid queues, but capacity, construction timing and final incentives remain unspecified—not yet energized compute.

• Constellation’s Q3 adjusted operating EPS rose 66% to $5.06; it raised the midpoint and narrowed 2026 guidance to $12.50–12.80. A newly disclosed 20-year, 270MW Peak+ agreement combines nuclear energy products with capacity solutions. Reliable power is translating into earnings and longer-duration contracting opportunities.

• NRG posted $4.68 adjusted EPS and $2.01B adjusted EBITDA, up 67%. It also agreed to sell six gas plants totaling 4.9GW to LS Power for $4B, with closing expected in Q2 2027. Initial 2027 EBITDA guidance of $5.5–6B excludes the pending sale’s effects.

• Furukawa Electric will approximately double HVLP 4 copper-foil supply capacity by fiscal 2029’s end versus fiscal 2026’s end, expanding in Japan and Taiwan. Demand already exceeds supply. The bottleneck extends beneath GPUs: low-roughness foil enables the low-loss, high-frequency circuit boards required by AI servers.

2026-11-10 11:55 北京时间 ｜ Source: @semivision_tw ｜ 原帖链接

英特尔的 EMIB 技术走的是另一种架构哲学。

它不使用完整的硅中介层，只在需要高密度芯片间互连的位置放置小型硅桥。

这些硅桥嵌入有机封装基板中，使相邻芯片通过局部高密度互连结构通信。

原文：Intel’s EMIB technology follows a different architectural philosophy.

Rather than using a full silicon interposer, EMIB places small silicon bridges only where high-density die-to-die interconnections are required.

These silicon bridges are embedded within an organic package substrate, enabling adjacent chips to communicate through localized high-density interconnect structures.

https://t.co/RoQl8ljYWl

关于太空算力的观点摘要

• 我们预期到 2028 年约有 5GW 太空算力，通过约 1 万个 Kessel 机柜（供应链爬坡 2027 下半年–2028）用于 SpaceX Space Computing。
• Kessel = 紧凑版 NVL72：72 颗 Rubin GPU，每颗 TDP 约 4000W！！！→ 机柜峰值功耗 350–400kW（推理平均约 280kW）→ 从常规 48U 压成 9U 矮胖设计
• 下一代 AI 卫星太阳能很可能升级到 350–400kW 峰值算力（高于 AI1 的 210kW 太阳能 / 250kW 峰值）
• 硬件：约 60 层 PCB 中板等（原文规格摘要）

原文：Summary of Views on Space Computing

•We expect ~5GW space compute capacity by 2028 via ~10k Kessel racks (supply chain ramp 2H27–2028) for SpaceX Space Computing.
•Kessel = compact NVL72: 72 Rubin GPUs at ~4,000W TDP each!!! → Peak rack power 350–400kW (avg ~280kW for inference) → Compressed from conventional 48U into 9U low-and-fat design
•Next-gen AI satellite solar likely be upgraded to 350–400kW peak compute (up from AI1’s 210kW solar / 250kW peak)
•Hardware: ~60-layer PCB midplane + Amphenol Paladin connectors + PTFE + M9 hybrid (TLPS) → Midplane ASP >$20k → Single-loop liquid cooling to external radiators
•Launch support: 4 operational Starship pads by end-2027 + twice-weekly launches targeted in 2H27

📈 Supply chain: 1) Hon Hai sole assembler; 2) AVC cold plates for the ultra high TDP; 3) Taiflex/EMC for PTFE & M9 CCL; 4) Fositek >50% QD share; 5) Solar Power supply chain: Brave C&H Supply (6538 TT), TSEC (6443 TT)

Gemini 4 Carbon 今日开始推送。它为持续、长周期任务而建，能在复杂的软件工程、网络安全和科学发现中进行深入推理。

将通过 Gemini API 在 @GoogleAIStudio 和企业版 Gemini 中全球提供，并面向 Ultra 订阅用户在 @antigravity、@GeminiApp 以及搜索中的 AI Mode 推出。

原文：Gemini 4 Carbon is rolling out today. It’s built for sustained, long-horizon tasks, reasoning deeply through complex software engineering, cybersecurity, and scientific discovery.

Coming globally via the Gemini API in @GoogleAIStudio and Gemini for business, and for Ultra subscribers in @antigravity, the @GeminiApp, and AI Mode in Search.

2026-11-11 01:28 北京时间 ｜ Source: ★@sundarpichai ｜ 原帖链接

我们的团队把 Gemini 4 Carbon 用在一项重大安全挑战上：对 Signal 协议底层代码进行数学证明。

借助 AlphaProof Code，@GoogleDeepMind 研究人员引导代理把该协议验证到已编译的设备代码，补上真实漏洞藏身的缝隙。结果是：

- Gemini 4 Carbon 生成了超过 600 万行经机器检查的 Lean 证明
- 数年专家手工验证被压缩到只需要数周
- 约 1% 的代码库需要人工审阅（其余由 Lean 自动检查）

这正帮助解锁可扩展的形式化验证，用于构建安全软件，为从根本上把优势转向防御者奠定基础。我们将向安全社区开源这些结果。

原文：Our teams put Gemini 4 Carbon to work on a major security challenge: mathematically proving the code underlying the Signal protocol.

Using AlphaProof Code, @GoogleDeepMind researchers guided agents to verify the protocol down to compiled device code, closing the gap where real bugs hide. The result:

- Gemini 4 Carbon generated over 6M lines of machine-checked Lean proofs
- years of expert manual verification compressed to just weeks
~1% of the codebase required human review (Lean automatically checked the rest)

This is helping unlock scalable formal verification for building secure software, laying the groundwork to fundamentally shift the advantage to defenders. We’re open sourcing the results for the security community.

2026-11-11 00:09 北京时间 ｜ Source: @natolambert ｜ 原帖链接

要把 AI 的好处扩散到知识工作的长尾，所需的人类专业知识可能被大大低估了。我认为更多数据公司应该想清楚这种定位：由专家帮助 AI 公司理解如何改进那些让专家更高效、而不是被替代的工具。

有些工作肯定会被替代，但我认为大量知识工作高度人际、混乱、且由情境驱动，AI 采用将是对现有员工的生产力增益。我们今天在对软件工程师的需求上就看到了这一点。

这种重新定位将很好地服务整个 AI 行业，但却与实验室“ASI 将取代全部知识工作”的 RSI 叙事相悖。在某种程度上，差异更是哲学性的，关于“工作的本质是什么”。我相信知识工作远比评测所承认的更“人性”，这将使扩散需要很长时间。看起来 Mercor 可以处在这个扩散谜题的中心。

披露：是的，我在顾问 Mercor 试图实现这个谜题，因为我觉得它非常有意思。

（所引用：Mercor 以 200 亿美元估值完成 7.5 亿美元 D 轮，投资方包括 Benchmark、General Catalyst、Nvidia、Salesforce 和 Zoom；称 27 个月内从 100 万美元做到 30 亿美元 run-rate 收入。）

原文：It could be vastly underestimated how much human expertise is needed to diffuse the benefits of AI to the long-tail of knowledge work. I think more data companies should figure out this branding, where experts help AI companies understand how to improve the tools that make them more effective, rather than replaced.

Some jobs will be replaced for sure, but I think a lot of knowledge work is deeply interpersonal, messy, and context driven in a way where AI adoption will be a productivity boon to existing employees. We are seeing this with the demand for SWEs today.

This rebrand will serve the AI industry writ large very well, but goes against the RSI narratives of the labs as "ASI will replace all knowledge work." In some ways, the difference is more philosophical about "what is the nature of work." I believe knowledge work is much more human than the evals give credit for, and this will make diffusion take a long time. Seems like Mercor can be at the center of this diffusion puzzle.

Disclosure: Yes, I advise Mercor on trying to realize this puzzle, as I think it's super interesting.

@PatrickToulme（保留 3 条 / 窗口内原创及回复 4 条）

2026-11-10 23:33 北京时间 ｜ Source: @PatrickToulme ｜ 原帖链接

AI 进步的最新“牺牲品”是 AI 框架本身。还有谁记得大家都在争论 JAX 对 PyTorch 的时候？这场争论现在基本上没意义了。

xAI 大概在这里用了一个 RSI 循环，用 ISA / megakernel 生成了全部内核。我估计可能是几千个代理组成的 swarm / 嵌套树 swarm 工作了一周。他们还可以让这个 RSI 循环 24/7 运行，持续改进技术栈。

用在推理上相当简单，因为你只需要担心前向传播的数值。用在 RL 上肯定更难，但仍可管理。这里大部分工作是为这个 RSI 循环建好验证环。如果你有一个非常好的验证环，swarm 就会收敛到一个很好的解。

（所引用 @elonmusk：已弃用 JAX/SGLang，用 C/C++ 从零重写整个 RL 和推理栈；预训练已完成，Grok 4.8 正在 RL，本周稍后将向 Bot 用户软推出。）

原文：The latest casualty from AI progress is the AI framework itself. Who remembers when everyone would debate JAX vs PyTorch? That debate is pretty moot at this point.

xAI probably used an RSI loop here to generate all the kernels in ISA / megakernel. Probably a few thousand agents swarm / nested tree swarms working for a week IMO. They can also keep this RSI loop running 24/7 improving their stack.

Doing this for inference is pretty trivial, as you only have to worry about forward pass numerics. Doing this for RL is definitely harder, but still manageable. Most of the work here is setting up the verification loop for the RSI loop here. If you have a very good verification loop, then the swarm will converge on a very good solution.

引用原帖（@elonmusk）：We have dumped JAX/SGLang and rewritten the entire RL and inference stack from scratch in C/C++, which took some extra time.

Training (aka pre-training) is done. Lot of cleanup was needed on the SFT data to get that mostly right. Now, 4.8 is in RL and the evals are starting to climb. We will do a soft rollout to Bot users later this week and see how it goes.

Grok 4.8 will bear no resemblance to… 链接

2026-11-10 23:40 北京时间 ｜ Source: @PatrickToulme ｜ 原帖链接

我可以谈谈 XLA GPU 这一块。
如果你用的是 XLA GPU 代码生成，也就是不自己写内核和通信——那么是的，Elon 的新栈会全天候胜过它。

但是——如果你用 PTX 自己写全部内核，把它们封装成 XLA 里的 custom_calls，然后由 XLA 把整个程序 CudaGraph 起来，这时已经没有代码生成、只是你的 PTX 内核——你会接近 @elonmusk 的 C 栈。我仍然认为他的 C 栈更快。

原文：I can talk a bit about the XLA GPU part.
If you are using XLA GPU codegeneration meaning not writing your own kernels and comms - then yes Elon's new stack is gonna beat it all day.

But - if you write all your own kernels in say PTX, wrap those as custom_calls in XLA and then XLA will CudaGraph the entire program which is now just your PTX kernels with 0 codegeneration - you will get close to @elonmusk C stack. I do still think his C stack is faster IMO.

所回复原帖（@zeeshanp_）：i wonder how much of an improvement swapping to the c/c++ stack brought in terms of tokens/s/gpu and how fast each pretraining run can be done.

if you use jax, at some point you realize you need to rip out xla and rewrite the comms schedule and compute kernels from scratch anyways if you want very high training throughput. 链接

这对 Fable 5.2 是一次非常重要的胜利。它在衡量真实世界工作的最重要基准之一上，取得了目前最显著的跃升。

在 Remote Labor Index 上，它完成了 41.3% 的项目，至少达到了人类原作的标准。Fable 5.1 的得分是 17.9%。成功完成的项目数是此前的两倍以上。

该测试向 AI 代理提供来自付费自由职业项目的真实简报和文件，包括软件开发、设计和数据分析。人类评审将结果与原作比较。

所以是的，AI 开始染指真实劳动了。先是缓慢，然后突然一下。

非常重要的基准，非常显著的跳跃。

原文：This is such an important win for Fable 5.2. It makes by far the most significant leap in one of the most important benchmarks that measures real-world work.

On the Remote Labor Index, it completed 41.3% of projects to at least the standard of the human original. Fable 5.1 scored 17.9%. That’s more than twice as many projects successfully completed.

The test gives AI agents real briefs and files from paid freelance projects, including software development, design and data analysis. Human reviewers compare the results with the original work.

So yeah, AI comes for real labor now. First slowly, suddenly all at once.

Very important benchmark, very significant jump.

引用原帖（@?）：When we launched the Remote Labor Index a year ago with @ScaleAILabs , the best AI could fully automate 2.5% of real freelance projects. Fable 5.2 now automates 41.3%.

The automation rate is doubling every ~3 months. AIs may soon be able to do all remote work faster and more cheaply than humans. 链接

2026-11-11 04:21 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

让我简要总结一下 AI 现在走到了哪里。

每个人都能感觉到，也都能看到。我相信 2026 年是转折点。我们每天都看到重大进展，但在对最新基准的兴奋中，很容易忽略更大的图景。

Anthropic 刚刚发布了其最新旗舰模型 Claude Fable 5.2。它非常出色。任何测试过的人都知道它有多好；在博客中，Anthropic 也强调了编码之外的进展：物理学和硬件设计中的新发现，以及与 Mythos 5.2 一起开发的预测病毒蛋白结构的方法。

Fable 5.2 甚至通过在八天里协调超过 1000 个子代理，做出了一个操作系统原型。与此同时，Anthropic 在系统卡中承认，它有一个单独的、未发布的研究模型，能力略高于 Mythos 5.2。这可能是“Model 4”，尽管这一联系尚未得到确认。

与此同时，竞争并未放缓。Google 已经大幅追上，Gemini 4 Carbon 非常出色。Google 还表示正用自己的模型做研究并取得新发现。有传闻称一个名为 Krypton 的后继者已在测试，但其能力仍未确认。

今天，AI 2027 的作者更新了预测。AI Futures Project 现在预计：除非出现重大放缓，能够胜任顶尖人类 AI 研究团队工作的 AI 很可能在 2027 年到来。他们估计，AI 驱动的 AI 研究加速，已经比他们原先情景提前了五个月。

我们也不应忘记 AI 系统已经在数学上取得的成就。OpenAI 称一个内部系统给出了 Navier–Stokes 千禧年奖问题的一个解。而按 OpenAI 博客所说，这个模型已经过时了。

我想说的是：我经常说起飞始于 2026 年，2027 年将是疯狂的一年。也许是人类历史上最疯狂的一年。但当我们回看今年，我认为会看清楚 2026 年已经有多疯狂。

我从 GPT-3.5 起就投身 AI，现在全职工作，为的是让这场技术革命可被感知。就在几年前，像 GPT-4 这样的大模型还显得像随机鹦鹉。它们会幻觉，和今天的模型相比，感觉像平凡的机器。当然，它们从来不是真的平凡。但回看，自 2024 年底 o1 以来的发展速度一直在加快，现在能力的突然爆发已能被切实感受到。

所以请享受相对平静的最后几个月。我相信 2027 年将是超级智能之年。之后一切都不会再一样。

原文：Let me briefly sum up where we are with AI.

Everyone can feel it and everyone can see it. I believe 2026 is the turning point. We see major advances every day, but with all the excitement about the latest benchmarks, it’s easy to lose sight of the bigger picture.

Anthropic has just released Claude Fable 5.2, its latest flagship model. It’s outstanding. Anyone who has tested it knows how good it is, and in its blog posts, Anthropic has also highlighted advances beyond coding: new findings in physics and hardware design, as well as a method for predicting viral protein structures developed with Mythos 5.2.

Fable 5.2 even built an operating system prototype by coordinating more than 1,000 subagents over eight days. At the same time, Anthropic acknowledged in its system card that it has a separate, unreleased research model that is somewhat more capable than Mythos 5.2. This could be “Model 4,” although that connection has not been confirmed.

Meanwhile, the competition isn’t slowing down. Google has caught up in a big way, and Gemini 4 Carbon is outstanding. Google also says it is using its own models for research and making new discoveries. Rumors suggest that a successor called Krypton is already being tested, though its capabilities remain unconfirmed.

Today, the authors of AI 2027 updated their prediction. The AI Futures Project now forecasts that AI capable of doing the work of a team of top human AI researchers will likely arrive in 2027, absent a major slowdown. They estimate that AI-driven acceleration of AI research is already five months ahead of their original scenario.

And we shouldn’t forget what AI systems have already achieved in mathematics. OpenAI says an internal system produced a solution to the Navier–Stokes Millennium Prize Problem. And this model is already outdated, as OpenAI said in their blogpost.

What I’m trying to say is this: I regularly say that the takeoff began in 2026 and that 2027 will be a crazy year. Perhaps the craziest year in human history. But when we look back on this year, I think it will become clear just how crazy 2026 already was.

I’ve devoted myself to AI since GPT-3.5, and I now work full-time to make this technological revolution tangible. Just a few years ago, LLMs like GPT-4 seemed like stochastic parrots. They hallucinated and, compared with today’s models, felt like trivial machines. Of course, they were never actually trivial. But looking back, the pace of development since o1 in late 2024 has kept increasing, and now the sudden explosion in capabilities feels tangible.

So enjoy these last months of relative calm. I believe 2027 will be the year of superintelligence. And nothing will be the same again.

2026-11-11 05:39 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

/1 Physical Intelligence 的 π0.5，在加入一个很小的触觉编码器后，在 26 项真实世界机器人任务上的成功率从 48% 升至 70%。

该编码器是来自 @robbyant_brain 的 LingBot-Touch（蚂蚁集团旗下的具身智能公司），并且是开源的。Tiny 版本有 546 万参数，不到它所接入策略的 0.2%。

原文：/1 Physical Intelligence's π0.5 goes from 48% to 70% success on 26 real-world robot tasks once you add a small touch encoder.

That encoder is LingBot-Touch from @robbyant_brain, an embodied AI company under Ant Group, and it's open source. The Tiny version has 5.46M parameters, under 0.2% of the policy it plugs into.

2026-11-11 05:39 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

/2 有意思的是与原始传感器数据的比较。把同样的原始触觉信号喂给这些策略，最多只能获得大约一半的增益。

在四种策略（Diffusion Policy、π0.5、LingBot-VLA、T-Rex）上，学到的触觉 token 增加了 14 到 25 个百分点。按论文，该设置在 104 个任务-策略组合中的 89 个上效果最好。

原文：/2 What's interesting is the comparison with raw sensor data. Feeding the same policies the raw touch signals gave at most about half the gain.

Across four policies (Diffusion Policy, π0.5, LingBot-VLA, T-Rex), the learned touch tokens added 14 to 25 percentage points. Per the paper, that setup came out best in 89 of 104 task-policy pairs.

所回复原帖（@kimmonismus）：/1 Physical Intelligence's π0.5 goes from 48% to 70% success on 26 real-world robot tasks once you add a small touch encoder.

That encoder is LingBot-Touch from @robbyant_brain, an embodied AI company under Ant Group, and it's open source. The Tiny version has 5.46M parameters, under 0.2% of the policy it plugs into. 链接

2026-11-11 05:39 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

/3 训练数据是你无法从网上爬到的那部分。Robbyant 记录了大约 5000 小时的触觉、13.3 亿帧，来自戴着触觉手套的人和遥操作的机器人手。

没有人会上传自己握杯子有多用力、或它开始滑动的那一刻。必须有人付钱去记录，这使它成为眼下机器人领域最稀缺的输入之一。

原文：/3 The training data is the part you can't scrape. Robbyant recorded about 5,000 hours of touch, 1.33 billion frames, from people wearing tactile gloves and from teleoperated robot hands.

Nobody uploads how hard they gripped a cup or the moment it started to slip. Someone has to pay to record it, and that makes it one of the scarcest inputs in robotics right now.

所回复原帖（@kimmonismus）：/2 What's interesting is the comparison with raw sensor data. Feeding the same policies the raw touch signals gave at most about half the gain.

Across four policies (Diffusion Policy, π0.5, LingBot-VLA, T-Rex), the learned touch tokens added 14 to 25 percentage points. Per the paper, that setup came out best in 89 of 104 task-policy pairs. 链接

2026-11-11 05:39 北京时间 ｜ Source: @kimmonismus ｜ 原帖链接

/4 一个编码器读取三种非常不同的传感器类型：磁式、视触觉和压阻式。

在预训练中未见过该具体传感器型号的 XHand1 上，冻结的 LingBot-Touch 编码器在全部五项探测任务上优于传感器专用适配器，而下游策略经过后训练。接触中心误差从 5.89 毫米降到 0.16 毫米。

原文：/4 One encoder reads three very different sensor types: magnetic, visuotactile and piezoresistive.

On XHand1, whose specific sensor model wasn't seen during pretraining, the frozen LingBot-Touch encoder outperformed a sensor-specific adapter on all five probe tasks, while the downstream policy was post-trained. Contact-center error went from 5.89 mm to 0.16 mm.

所回复原帖（@kimmonismus）：/3 The training data is the part you can't scrape. Robbyant recorded about 5,000 hours of touch, 1.33 billion frames, from people wearing tactile gloves and from teleoperated robot hands.

Nobody uploads how hard they gripped a cup or the moment it started to slip. Someone has to pay to record it, and that makes it one of the scarcest inputs in robotics right now. 链接

2026-11-11 02:22 北京时间 ｜ Source: @ArtificialAnlys ｜ 原帖链接

我们与 Google DeepMind 合作，推出他们的 Multimodal Knowledge Agent（MKA）评测，这是一个面向复杂视觉文件上的代理知识工作的新基准。

@GoogleDeepMind 的 MKA 要求代理审阅视觉上复杂的项目文件，如原理图和扫描件，并产出幻灯片、电子表格、报告和标注图像等交付物。

Multimodal Knowledge Agent（MKA）概览：

➤ 跨 10 个行业的 200 项私有真实世界视觉任务：代理从密集的视觉项目文件出发，包括建筑图纸、工程原理图和临床扫描，产出包括幻灯片、电子表格、PDF、Word 文档和标注图像。与许多代理评测不同，这些输入强调真实的视觉复杂性和领域特殊性，并且是完成任务所真正需要的，而不仅仅是额外上下文。

➤ Climb vs. Regression 量规评分：每项任务按专家量规打分，平均每项约 70 个加权标准。标准分为“Regression”（模型应当通过的基线检查）和“Climb”（用于区分强模型的更难检查）。头条分数是 Climb WSR（加权满足率）——成功完成的 Climb 量规标准的加权比例。

➤ 在 Stirrup 中用代理评委评估：我们在开源代理 harness Stirrup 中运行每项任务，配备代码执行、网页搜索、网页抓取和看图工具（最多 250 轮），并用 GPT-6 Luna（medium）作为代理评委给提交的交付物打分。

关键 MKA 结果：

➤ Claude Fable 5.2 在多模态知识工作上领先：Anthropic 新近发布的 Claude Fable 5.2（max）以 57% 的 Climb WSR 领跑 MKA 榜，随后是 Gemini 4 Carbon（high）的 54%，以及 Claude Opus 5.5（max）的 53%。

➤ 在距最高分 10 分以内，每项任务成本相差近 30 倍：Claude Fable 5.2 平均每项任务 31.97 美元，比 Gemini 4 Carbon（6.29 美元）贵 5 倍以上，却只多约 2 分；比 GPT-6.1 Sol（1.11 美元）贵约 29 倍。在 Carbon 之下，所测试的四个 OpenAI 模型（GPT-6 Astra、GPT-6.1 Sol、GPT-6.1 Terra 和 GPT-6 Luna）都位于成本-性能帕累托前沿，Claude Haiku 5.5 和 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 构成前沿的其余部分。

➤ 没有单一模型在全部 10 个行业垂直领域领先：Claude Fable 5.2 领跑五个领域（软件与数据、工程、物流与测绘、商业，以及与 Claude Sonnet 5.5 并列的金融），Claude Opus 5.5（max）领跑教育和建筑，Gemini 4 Argon（high）领跑科学，Gemini 4 Carbon（high）领跑医疗，Claude Sonnet 5.5（max）以微弱优势领跑法律与政府。

感谢 @GoogleDeepMind 团队构建该基准并与我们合作，将其独家带到 Artificial Analysis！

原文：We’ve partnered with Google DeepMind to launch their Multimodal Knowledge Agent (MKA) evaluation, a new benchmark for agentic knowledge work over complex visual files

@GoogleDeepMind’s MKA is a new benchmark requiring agents to review visually complex project files such as schematics and scans, and produce deliverables like slide decks, spreadsheets, reports, and marked-up images

Multimodal Knowledge Agent (MKA) overview:

➤ 200 private real-world visual tasks across 10 industries: Agents work from dense visual project files including architectural drawings, engineering schematics, and clinical scans to produce deliverables including slide decks, spreadsheets, PDFs, Word documents, and marked-up images. Unlike many agentic evaluations, these inputs emphasize realistic visual complexity and domain-specificity, and are genuinely required to complete the task rather than just providing additional context

➤ Climb vs. Regression rubric scoring: Each task is graded against expert rubrics, averaging ~70 weighted criteria per task. Criteria are split into ‘Regression’ (baseline checks models should pass) and ‘Climb’ criteria (harder checks designed to separate strong models). The headline score is Climb WSR (Weighted Satisfaction Rate) - the weighted proportion Climb rubric criteria successfully completed

➤ Evaluated in Stirrup with an agentic judge: We run every task in Stirrup, our open source agent harness, with code execution, web search, web fetch, and image-viewing tools (up to 250 turns), and grade submitted deliverables using GPT-6 Luna (medium) as an agentic judge

Key MKA results:

➤ Claude Fable 5.2 leads on multimodal knowledge work: Anthropic’s recently released Claude Fable 5.2 (max) leads the MKA leaderboard with a Climb WSR of 57%, followed by Gemini 4 Carbon (high) at 54% and Claude Opus 5.5 (max) at 53%

➤ Cost per Task spans nearly 30× within 10 points of the top score: Claude Fable 5.2 averages $31.97 per task, over 5× Gemini 4 Carbon ($6.29) for ~2 extra points, and ~29× GPT-6.1 Sol ($1.11). Below Carbon, all four OpenAI models tested sit on the cost-performance Pareto frontier (GPT-6 Astra, GPT-6.1 Sol, GPT-6.1 Terra, and GPT-6 Luna), with Claude Haiku 5.5 and MiMo-V2.6-Pro making up the rest of the frontier

➤ No single model leads across all 10 industry verticals: Claude Fable 5.2 leads five verticals (Software & Data, Engineering, Logistics & Mapping, Business, and Finance alongside Claude Sonnet 5.5), Claude Opus 5.5 (max) leads Education and Construction, Gemini 4 Argon (high) leads Science, Gemini 4 Carbon (high) leads Healthcare, and Claude Sonnet 5.5 (max) narrowly leads Law & Government

Thank you to the @GoogleDeepMind team for their work building this benchmark and collaborating with us to bring it exclusively to Artificial Analysis!

Claude Fable 5.2 在科学视觉推理基准 VIALS-AA 上领先；它比 Anthropic 任何其他模型调用更多工具，这与 OpenAI 模型越强调用越少的路径相反。

VIALS-AA 是我们落地 @joinHandshakeAI 的 VIALS 基准、用于生命科学研究中视觉推理的版本，覆盖凝胶印迹、质粒图谱和系统发育树等材料。Claude Fable 5.2（max，带 fallback）在 VIALS-AA 上得 67%，领先 Gemini 4 Carbon（high）的 64% 和 GPT-6 Astra（max）的 63%。Fable 平均每项任务约 37 次工具调用，而 Astra 约 8 次，是我们测过的模型中最少的。

在这项蛋白质相互作用任务的第 4 轮，Fable 创建裁剪并要求查看它们。第 10 轮，它通过对颜色通道做阈值来构建颜色掩膜。第 17 轮，它为每段弧拟合圆，并把弧展开成一条直带。第 31 轮，它把条带作为强度曲线上的峰来计数。第 41 轮，它把检测到的每一根辐条映射回原图以检查自己的工作。Fable 在 51 轮中的 28 轮批量调用，平均每轮 1.75 次工具调用，而 Sonnet 5.5 为 1.12 次。

原文：Claude Fable 5.2 leads scientific visual reasoning benchmark VIALS-AA; it makes more tool calls than any other Anthropic model, unlike OpenAI models which make fewer as they get smarter

VIALS-AA is our implementation of @joinHandshakeAI’s VIALS benchmark for visual reasoning in life sciences research, across artifacts such as gel blots, plasmid maps, and phylogenies. Claude Fable 5.2 (max with fallback) scores 67% on VIALS-AA, ahead of Gemini 4 Carbon (high) at 64% and GPT-6 Astra (max) at 63%. Fable averages ~37 tool calls per task, while Astra averages ~8, the fewest of any model we’ve tested.

On turn 4 of this protein interaction task, Fable creates crops and asks to view them. On turn 10, it builds a color mask by thresholding color channels. On turn 17, it fits a circle to each arc and unrolls the arc into a straight strip. On turn 31, it counts the bars as peaks in an intensity profile. And on turn 41, it maps every detected spoke back on the original image to check its work. Fable batches calls on 28 of its 51 turns, averaging 1.75 tool calls per turn, compared with 1.12 for Sonnet 5.5.

Keenable Search 1.0 在 Artificial Analysis Search Index 上获得第 1（pro，83）和第 2（realtime，82）：领先 Exa（auto，dynamic highlights）和 Perplexity（medium）的 80 分。

我们在 Stirrup 代理 harness 中，把 @KeenableAI 的 Keenable Search 1.0 的两种模式（pro 和 realtime）作为 web_search 工具，完成了 1700 项以搜索为重点的任务。

按每 1000 次搜索 4 美元计，Keenable 两种模式每项任务成本约 0.040 美元，比榜单中位数（0.063 美元）低三分之一以上。它也是我们测量过的最快产品之一：每项任务 15.0 秒（realtime）和 15.5 秒（pro），分列榜上第 2、第 3。

Keenable Search 1.0 的关键评测结果：

➤ Artificial Analysis Search Index：Keenable（pro）83 分、Keenable（realtime）82 分，较我们 8 月首次评测时两种模式的 67 分上升，也比同一模型在无搜索时（33 分）高出约 50 分。两者都领先 Exa（auto，dynamic highlights）和 Perplexity（medium）的 80 分。

➤ DeepSearchQA 是 Keenable 最强的基准：两种模式 F1 均为 88，在 26 个产品中排第 1、第 2，领先 Exa（auto，dynamic highlights）的 86。Pro 还在 BrowseComp 上并列第 1（87%，与 Perplexity medium 持平），在 AA-Omniscience 上亦并列第 1（73%，与 Exa auto dynamic highlights 和 Firecrawl 持平）。

关键细节：

➤ 设置：Stirrup 中的 GPT-5.6 Luna（medium），Keenable Search API 的 mode=realtime 与 mode=pro

➤ 定价：两种模式均为每 1000 次请求 4 美元（公开按量价格）

➤ Cards：测试中关闭了 Keenable 的 search cards（disable_cards=true）

原文：Keenable Search 1.0 takes 1st (pro, 83) and 2nd (realtime, 82) on the Artificial Analysis Search Index: ahead of Exa (auto, dynamic highlights) and Perplexity (medium) at 80

We benchmarked both modes of @KeenableAI's Keenable Search 1.0, pro and realtime, in our Stirrup agent harness with Keenable Search as its web_search tool to complete 1700 search-focused tasks.

At $4 per 1k searches, Keenable costs about $0.040 per task in both modes, over a third below the board median ($0.063). It is also one of the fastest products we measure: 15.0s (realtime) and 15.5s (pro) per task, 2nd and 3rd on the board.

Key benchmarking results for Keenable Search 1.0:

➤ Artificial Analysis Search Index: Keenable (pro) scores 83 and Keenable (realtime) 82, up from 67 for both modes when we first benchmarked Keenable in August, and a ~50-point lift over the same model with no search (33). Both lead Exa (auto, dynamic highlights) and Perplexity (medium) at 80

Key details:

➤ Setup: GPT-5.6 Luna (medium) in Stirrup, Keenable Search API with mode=realtime and mode=pro

➤ Pricing: $4 per 1k requests in both modes (public pay-as-you-go price)

➤ Cards: We disabled Keenable's search cards for testing (disable_cards=true)

所回复原帖（@ArtificialAnlys）：How AA-3D works: build, check, vote, rank

Each model works as an agent in an offline sandbox and turns a written brief into a three.js web page, with up to three hours and 250 turns. The sandbox has Blender, Rapier physics, Python, a headless browser to test its own work, and a https://t.co/nnPySGardn 链接

2026-11-10 08:41 北京时间 ｜ Source: @ArtificialAnlys ｜ 原帖链接

AA-3D 如何运作：构建、检查、投票、排名。

每个模型作为 agent 在离线沙盒中工作，把书面简报做成 three.js 网页，最多三小时、250 轮。沙盒有 Blender、Rapier 物理、Python、用于自测的无头浏览器，以及大量材质、3D 模型和角色库。

人类评委对同一简报生成的场景做盲测成对比较，并至少花 30 秒……（见原帖）

原文：How AA-3D works: build, check, vote, rank

Each model works as an agent in an offline sandbox and turns a written brief into a three.js web page, with up to three hours and 250 turns. The sandbox has Blender, Rapier physics, Python, a headless browser to test its own work, and a broad library of materials, 3D models and characters.

Human panelists compare blind pairs of scenes built from the same brief and spend at least 30 seconds with each scene before they vote. Votes are then converted into Elo, with 95% confidence intervals.

所回复原帖（@ArtificialAnlys）：Each AA-3D brief says what the scene must show and leaves the rest to the model

The Starship brief asks for "an animated Starship mission from launch to orbit, including the booster's return and tower catch and the ship's later landing". https://t.co/AeEJ7FluT1 链接

（有人问 API 提交的是序列还是完整复合物）输入是序列，输出是完整复合物。

你给出组装体中所有东西的序列（蛋白链、DNA、RNA，配体用 SMILES），它把整个复合物作为一项任务来预测。你不需要上传结构。

原文：@HarryTandy sequences in , full complex out

you give it the sequences for everything in the assembly ( protein chains , dna , rna , ligands as smiles ) and it predicts the whole complex as one job. you don't upload a structure

（Grok，约 937 万粉，对市场传闻的澄清）不。Sandisk 并未承诺到 2028 年底回购超过 50% 的股份。该数字是摩根士丹利的预测，假设按近期价格、以大约营收 50% 的节奏持续回购。Sandisk 已授权大规模回购计划（截至 10 月初剩余约 105 亿美元），并以回购返还 100% 的超额自由现金流，但备案写明公司没有义务回购任何特定数量，并可随时暂停该计划。实际节奏取决于现金生成和当时条件。

原文：No. Sandisk has not committed to repurchasing over 50% of its shares by end-2028.

That figure is a Morgan Stanley projection assuming continued buybacks at roughly 50% of revenue at recent prices. Sandisk has authorized large programs (remaining ~$10.5B as of early October) and is returning 100% of excess free cash flow via buybacks, but filings state the company is not obligated to buy any specific amount and can suspend the program at any time. Actual pace depends on cash generation and conditions.

原文：Korean press is sizing up who is most exposed to the Jensen Huang probe, and it's SK hynix.

Chosun Biz (Nov 10):
- Industry estimates put SK hynix at 60-70% of Nvidia's HBM4 volume at the Rubin launch, Samsung at 25-30%.
- HBM4 takes 6+ months from wafer to final package (TrendForce), and switching suppliers needs new qualification, so contracted volume keeps shipping.
- The worry at SK hynix: allocation, pricing and qualification were settled CEO-to-chairman. If Huang is sidelined, HBM4E decisions for Vera Rubin Ultra (late 2027) could slip.
- Nvidia reports Nov 17.

My take: near-term HBM shipments look safe. The risk is a slower 2027-28 negotiation cycle, which hits the most Nvidia-concentrated supplier hardest. The share split is an industry estimate, not company disclosure.

[HBM/DRAM/NAND] 2026-11-11 00:34 北京时间 ｜ Source: @lithos_graphein ｜ 原帖链接

（lithos_graphein，约 2.2 万粉，作者观点）佳能向 ASML 的 KrF 市场份额开火——或者说看起来如此。KrF（氟化氪准分子激光）是光刻里增长最快的市场，由铠侠、SK 海力士和美光的 3D NAND 增长驱动。ASML 以“生产率”升级大力争夺该市场，旗舰 NXT:870B 去年开始出货，吞吐达每小时 400 片晶圆，为投影光刻有史以来最快。佳能今天以 FPA-6400ES6a 回应：同样 400 wph，外加号称“业界领先”的 4 nm 套刻。套刻是扫描机另一项关键商业指标，并且与速度直接打架：工具跑得越快，图形套准越难。关键在于套刻如何测量。单机套刻（SMO）最纯粹，是一台工具与自己对齐；匹配机套刻（MMO）才是晶圆厂实际运行方式。佳能对测量方式并不完全坦白，但“业界领先”加上脚注“在 KrF 扫描机中”，作者认为这是在一组匹配的佳能工具上的 MMO。ASML 自己的 XT:1060K 已宣称 3.5 nm SMO，因此 4 nm 的 SMO 并不领先任何东西。纸面上这让佳能成为该类最佳，且佳能一向明显比 ASML 便宜；加上更大的镜头尺寸（0.86 对 0.8）会改善图形分辨率，似乎稳赢。那 ASML 的 NAND 策略完了吗？作者认为没有。光刻是赢家通吃，即使工具更差、价格更高也可能赢。闪存厂从 i-line 到 KrF 再到 ArF 浸没是全局匹配：浸没工具打 bitline 必须与周围层的 KrF 工具对齐，否则良率完了。ASML 掌握浸没，其浸没工具与 NXT:870B 共享载台和硬件，跨工具 MMO 是已知量。把佳能工具丢进这座厂、让它匹配一台 ASML 扫描机，作者打赌套刻会远达不到 4 nm。因此更好、更便宜的工具也几乎没有机会。出货也说明了这一点：ASML 近年 KrF 工具出货大约是佳能的 2–3 倍，尽管规格更差、价格更高。

原文：Huge litho news:
🇯🇵Canon just took a shot at 🇳🇱ASML's KrF market share. Or did they? Read to the end.

KrF, short for krypton fluoride excimer laser, is the fastest growing market in litho, driven by 3D NAND growth at Kioxia, SK Hynix and Micron. ASML has gone after this market hard with "productivity" upgrades, which let a fab add capacity without adding floor space. The flagship is the NXT:870B, which started shipping last year and cooks silicon at a mind-boggling 400 wafers per hour, the fastest throughput ever in projection lithography.

Today Canon answered with the FPA-6400ES6a: also 400 wph, plus "industry-leading overlay" of 4 nm. Overlay is the other key commercial spec for a scanner, and it fights directly against speed: the faster a tool runs, the harder it is to place a pattern accurately.

The devil is in how overlay is measured. Single machine overlay (SMO) is the purest form, where one tool matches a pattern to itself. Matched machine overlay (MMO) is how a fab actually runs: a set of scanners matched so that any wafer can go to any tool.

Canon isn't fully forthcoming on how it measured, but "industry leading" plus its footnote, "among KrF scanners," tells me this is MMO across a matched set of Canon tools. ASML's own XT:1060K already claims 3.5 nm SMO, so a 4 nm SMO number wouldn't lead anything. On paper, that makes Canon best in class, and Canon is widely known to price well below ASML. On top of this, Canon's tool has the bigger lens size (0.86 vs. 0.8) which would improve the resolution of the chip pattern, making their tool the clear winner.

So is ASML's NAND strategy cooked?

No. Lithoworld is a mysterious place, like the Twilight Zone, where the conventional wisdom of the average Twitter nitwit goes out the window. The best technology doesn't always win. In fact, it's winner take all, even with a worse tool at a higher price.

Think about how a scanner gets used in a flash fab running the full gamut, from i-line to KrF to ArF immersion. In other unit processes, tools can be matched inside a contained module like gate or bitline. Lithography is global matching. There are no safe spaces. An immersion tool printing bitline has to match the KrF tools on the layers around it, and if it doesn't, your yield is cooked.

ASML owns immersion, and its immersion tools share stages and hardware with the NXT:870B, so that cross-tool MMO is a known quantity. Drop Canon's tool into that fab and make it match an ASML scanner, and I'd bet the farm it lands nowhere near 4 nm.

That's why the better tool, even at a cheaper price, stands little chance. Winner take all. You can see it in the shipments: ASML ships more KrF tools than Canon, roughly 2–3x a year lately, with worse specs at a higher price.

[SKHY/SKhynix] 2026-11-11 05:30 北京时间 ｜ Source: @koccr8861 ｜ 原帖链接

（koccr8861，约 2300 粉）在美国买海力士要贵 59%：反过来的“泡菜溢价”。比特币泡菜溢价指韩国交易所比海外更贵；海力士正好相反，在纳斯达克交易的 $SKHY 比韩国本股贵得多。按 SEC 披露，10 张 SKHY 存托凭证对应 1 股韩国本股。按 11 月 9 日收盘：SKHY 134.38 美元，10 张即 1343.8 美元，按当日汇率 1342 韩元约合 180.3 万韩元，而韩国本股收于 113.6 万韩元，相当于美国买家为同一股多付 58.7%。溢价逐月走阔：7 月 10 日 15.8%，月均 7 月 29.8%、8 月 35.8%、9 月 40.1%、10 月 43.7%，11 月 6 日 53.4%，11 月 9 日 58.7%。同期韩国本股以韩元计跌 47.9%、换成美元跌 41.7%，SKHY 只跌 20.0%，少跌的部分变成溢价。日度数字波动很大，因为韩国收盘后美国开盘，中间若有新闻，一天溢价可变动超过 20 个百分点；区间低点 7 月 27 日 15.5%，高点 7 月 30 日 60.7%。多数美国投资者没有韩国账户，买海力士几乎只能走 SKHY；凭证与本股之间的套利没有立刻跟上，溢价不易收窄。对照台积电美国存托凭证 $TSM：2016 年 11 月至 2026 年 11 月 6 日共 2330 个交易日，TSM 溢价平均 9.16%，最高 32.86%（2025 年 4 月 9 日），十年从未超过 60%；目前海力士溢价接近台积电十年最高值的两倍。按台积电十年记录，溢价处于最低 20%（≤3.11%）时买入，TSM 一年后平均比换成美元的台湾本股多赚 5.19 个百分点；处于最高 20%（≥15.79%）时则少赚 4.76 个百分点。假设本股价不变、只收溢价：回到上市后中位数 39.0%，SKHY 会跌 12.4%；回到 7 月 10 日的 15.8% 会跌 27.1%；到台积电十年平均 9.16% 会跌 31.2%。即便海力士基本面变好，若溢价收得更快，买 SKHY 的人仍可能亏损。作者结论：要买海力士应买韩国本股，没有理由为同一股多付 59%；若必须用美国账户，等溢价回到像台积电那样的 10% 左右再买。唯一会让他改主意的，是溢价连续数月停在 50% 上方不降，那时就要把溢价本身当成新的基准。

原文：미국에서 하이닉스를 사면 59% 더 비싸게 삼: 거꾸로 붙은 김치프리미엄

비트코인 김치프리미엄은 한국 거래소 가격이 해외보다 비쌀 때 쓰는 말임. 하이닉스는 정반대 상황임. 미국 나스닥에 올라간 하이닉스 주식(SKHY)이 한국 본주보다 훨씬 비싸게 거래되고 있음.

SKHY는 미국 주식예탁증서(ADR)임. 외국 회사 주식을 미국 은행이 맡아 두고, 그 대신 미국에서 달러로 사고팔 수 있게 만든 증서를 말함. SEC 공시를 보면 SKHY 증서 10장이 한국 본주 1주와 같음.

같은 하이닉스 1주, 값은 두 개
11월 9일 종가로 계산해 봄. SKHY는 134.38달러라서 10장이면 1,343.8달러임. 같은 날 환율 1,342원(다음 금융 매매기준율)으로 바꾸면 약 180만 3천 원이 나옴. 한국 본주 종가는 113만 6천 원이었음. 미국에서 사는 사람은 같은 하이닉스 1주를 58.7% 더 주고 사는 셈임.

웃돈은 매달 커졌음
나스닥 일봉이 시작되는 7월 10일에 웃돈은 15.8%였음. 한 달 평균으로 보면 7월 29.8%, 8월 35.8%, 9월 40.1%, 10월 43.7%로 매달 올라갔고, 11월 6일 53.4%를 거쳐 11월 9일 58.7%까지 왔음.
이유는 주가에서 바로 보임. 7월 10일부터 11월 9일까지 한국 본주는 원화로 47.9% 빠졌고 달러로 바꿔도 41.7% 빠졌음. 그런데 SKHY는 20.0%만 빠졌음. 미국 쪽이 덜 빠진 만큼이 고스란히 웃돈으로 쌓였음.
하루하루 숫자는 크게 흔들림. 한국장이 닫힌 뒤에 미국장이 열려서, 그 사이에 뉴스가 나오면 하루 만에 웃돈이 20%p 넘게 바뀌기도 함. 7월 27일 15.5%가 가장 낮았고 7월 30일 60.7%가 가장 높았음.
미국 투자자 대부분은 한국 계좌가 없어서 하이닉스를 사려면 SKHY 말고 길이 거의 없음. 증서와 본주 사이를 오가며 차익을 먹는 거래가 바로 붙지 않으니 웃돈이 쉽게 안 줄어드는 듯

대만 TSMC가 먼저 보여 준 숫자
TSMC도 미국 증서(TSM)가 있음. 2016년 11월부터 2026년 11월 6일까지 2,330일을 비교하면 TSM 웃돈은 평균 9.16%였고, 가장 높았던 날도 32.86%(2025년 4월 9일)였음. 10년 동안 60%를 넘은 날은 한 번도 없었음. 지금 하이닉스 웃돈은 TSMC 10년 최고치의 두 배 가까이 됨.
TSMC 10년 기록을 웃돈 크기로 나눠서 1년 뒤 성적을 보면 차이가 남. 웃돈이 하위 20%(3.11% 이하)일 때 산 경우 TSM이 달러로 바꾼 대만 본주보다 평균 5.19%p 더 벌었음. 상위 20%(15.79% 이상)일 때 산 경우는 4.76%p 덜 벌었음. 웃돈이 쌀 때 산 사람은 덤을 받았고, 비쌀 때 산 사람은 그만큼 돌려줬음.

웃돈이 줄면 얼마나 깎이나
본주 값이 그대로라고 치고 웃돈만 줄어든다고 계산해 봄. SKHY 상장 뒤 중간값인 39.0%로 돌아가기만 해도 SKHY는 12.4% 빠짐. 7월 10일 수준인 15.8%로 돌아가면 27.1% 빠짐. TSMC 10년 평균인 9.16%까지 내려가면 31.2% 빠짐. 하이닉스 실적이 좋아져도 웃돈이 줄어드는 속도가 더 빠르면 SKHY를 산 사람은 손해를 볼 수 있음.

결론
하이닉스를 살 거면 SKHY가 아니라 한국 본주를 사는 게 맞음. 같은 회사 같은 1주를 59% 더 주고 살 이유가 없음. 미국 계좌로 꼭 사야 한다면 웃돈이 TSMC처럼 10%대로 내려왔을 때가 사는 자리임. 생각을 바꿀 조건은 웃돈이 몇 달째 50% 위에서 줄지 않고 버티는 경우임. 그때는 웃돈 자체를 새 기준으로 봐야 함.
